Abstract: Long-context reasoning is essential for complex and long-horizon tasks, yet the performance of large language models (LLMs) degrades as context length increases. Recent approaches address this by processing input chunk by chunk while maintaining a bounded textual memory in model context. However, premature information compression can discard critical details essential for subsequent reasoning. In this paper, we introduce Commit-on-Evidence Memory (CoEM), which learns when to convert source evidence into compact memory facts. Specifically, under a fixed context-memory budget, CoEM preserves potentially useful source excerpts verbatim in a pending set, allowing subsequent context to clarify their relevance before irreversible compression. As new context arrives, a learned policy revisits each pending excerpt and decides whether to promote it to the committed memory, retain it for further consideration, or discard it. A frozen verifier ensures proposed facts are accepted only if supported by retained excerpts and current context. To further guide effective memory management, we train this policy using reinforcement learning by combining fine-grained, step-level evidence rewards with final answer rewards. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CoEM consistently improves long-context reasoning. When evaluated on 6,400 documents long-context input, CoEM outperforms the strongest memory baseline by 10.4-11.4 F1 points on Qwen3.5-9B. Code repository: https://github.com/benmagnifico/CoEM.

</details>


### [105] [SCA: Spatial Credit Assignment for Reinforcement Learning of GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36939)
*Shengtian Yang,Ziyu Xiong,Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Yewen Li,Peng Jiang,Gai Kun,Qingpeng Cai,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Spatial Credit Assignment (SCA)方法，通过利用点击坐标的空间信息优化组相对强化学习中的信用分配，解决二元评估无法区分不同失败情况的问题，显著提升了GUI代理的指令接地和行动预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的组相对强化学习方法将空间上不同的错误点击视为相同的负反馈，且在所有采样点击均失败时缺乏相对信号，导致策略更新不够精细。

Method: 提出SPatial Credit Assignment (SCA)。具体而言：1）对于包含成功和失败的组，利用其他响应预测被留出响应的奖励，并通过预测残差调整信用；2）当所有采样点击均失败时，根据距离标注目标的远近对它们进行排序以提供相对信号。该方法仅用于构建训练更新，不改变部署时的策略。

Result: 在受控合成研究中验证了修正后的策略更新与精确回报梯度的误差和方向对齐性得到改善。在GUI接地和离线行动预测基准测试中，SCA在各个专业领域的接地任务上均有提升，并在大多数行动预测指标上实现了最强的性能，表现优于其他经过强化微调的模型。

Conclusion: 利用屏幕坐标的空间参考信息 refine 组相对信用分配，能有效克服现有方法的局限性，显著提升GUI智能体的行动预测精度和地面实况接地能力。

Abstract: GUI agents automate tasks on digital devices by grounding language instructions in visual interfaces. Existing group-relative reinforcement learning improves GUI action prediction by comparing the rewards of multiple responses sampled from the same GUI state. However, binary evaluation treats spatially different failed clicks as identical and provides no relative signal when all sampled clicks fail. To address these limitations, we propose Spatial Credit Assignment (SCA), which uses the screen coordinates of sampled clicks to refine group-relative credit. Specifically, SCA predicts each held-out response's reward from the other responses in groups containing both successes and failures, then uses the prediction residual to adjust credit. When all sampled clicks fail, SCA instead orders them by distance to the annotated target. These spatial references are used only to construct the training update; the deployed policy remains unchanged. We evaluate whether this correction improves the policy update itself by comparing its error and directional alignment with the exact return gradient in a controlled synthetic study. Across GUI grounding and offline action-prediction benchmarks, SCA improves grounding across professional domains and achieves the strongest results among reinforcement-fine-tuned models on most action-prediction metrics, with consistent gains across the reported GUI suites.

</details>


### [106] [Dual-Channel Robust Group-Relative Policy Optimization via Advantage and Sequence-Weight Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36944)
*Zhongyi Li,Wan Tian,Xiang Xu,Yutian Xiao,Yikun Ban,Yijie Peng,Fuzhen Zhuang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RoVR-GSPO，一种双通道鲁棒优化器，通过结合稳健参考估计、有界残差信用和可微分SoftRoVR聚合，解决群体相对策略优化（GSPO）中因极端奖励和token级比率扰动导致的敏感性问题，显著提升了数学推理、长文本摘要等任务的性能与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的群体相对策略优化（GSPO）依赖基于奖励的优势度和序列级似然权重，这两者对局部异常值极为敏感。极端奖励会导致清洗后的响应在组归一化后差异崩溃，而token级别的log-ratio扰动会改变序列权重及裁剪决策，从而降低模型稳定性。

Method: 引入RoVR-GSPO作为双通道鲁棒优化器：1. 奖励通道：结合稳健的参考估计与有界残差信用；2. 比率通道：使用可微分的SoftRoVR聚合构建鲁棒的序列权重。同时提供了两个通道的稳定性和效率分析。

Result: 在数学推理、长上下文摘要和工具调用标注任务上，相比GSPO展现出一致的改进；受控扰动研究表明该方法对奖励污染和token比率异常具有更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: RoVR-GSPO通过分离处理奖励和比率通道的异常问题，有效解决了GSPO对局部异常值敏感的缺陷，证明了其在多种NLP任务中的优越性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Group-relative policy optimization relies on reward-derived advantages and sequence-level likelihood weights, both of which can be sensitive to localized outliers. Extreme rewards can collapse the contrast among clean responses after group normalization, while token-level log-ratio perturbations can alter sequence weights and clipping decisions. We introduce RoVR-GSPO, a dual-channel robust optimizer that addresses these failure modes separately. Its reward channel combines robust reference estimation with bounded residual credit, while its ratio channel uses differentiable SoftRoVR aggregation to construct robust sequence weights. We provide stability and efficiency analyses for both channels. Experiments on mathematical reasoning, long-context summarization, and tool-call annotation show consistent improvements over GSPO, while controlled perturbation studies demonstrate stronger robustness to reward contamination and token-ratio anomalies.

</details>


### [107] [REALHOP: Rethinking Multi-Hop Reasoning Evaluation via Behavioral Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36984)
*Jiawen Tao,Xiaokun Yuan,Yaoming Li,Chenxu Liu,Mengzhou Wu,Tong Yang,Maxm Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出仅凭答案正确性无法证明模型具备真正的多跳推理能力，提出了BEHAVIORAL NECESSITY RATE (BNR) 来量化对证据的依赖性，并设计了REALHOP框架重构基准测试，显著提升了对意图推理链的证据依赖性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多跳推理基准测试通常假设正确答案意味着使用了预定义的推理链，但模型可能通过记忆关联、短路径或部分证据获得正确答案，而非真正依赖每一步的证据，导致评估结果存在偏差。

Method: 提出行为必要性率（BNR）衡量删除目标证据后回答失败的比例；构建REALHOP框架，通过重新绑定实体、分解关系、增加竞争路径和定位证据，结合结构和语义检查及行为干预，重构MuSiQue等基准数据。

Result: 在REALHOP增强后的测试集中，面板均值BNR从27.4%大幅提升至94.4%，同时保持高准确率；在REALHOP-FRAMES和REALHOP-LONGBENCH上也实现高BNR；长上下文问题中模型间表现差距显著扩大且具有一致性。

Conclusion: 概念连贯的推理链和最终答案正确并不足以确立多跳推理能力，必须验证成功是否依赖于每一个预期的推理步骤，这应作为多跳评估的核心环节，与答案准确性同等重要。

Abstract: Complex questions often require multi-hop reasoning that connects facts distributed across sources or distant regions of a long context through intermediate steps. Benchmarks commonly evaluate this ability with questions built around predefined reasoning chains, treating a correct answer as evidence that the intended composition was used. Yet answer correctness alone leaves open whether success depends on the evidence associated with each intended step: models may instead rely on memorized associations, shorter paths, or partial evidence. We examine this dependence using the Behavioral Necessity Rate (BNR), which measures how often targeted evidence removal prevents answer recovery on initially correct instances. Across five existing benchmarks, panel-mean BNR ranges from 16.6% to 48.9%, exposing a substantial gap between annotated structure and observed dependence. Guided by this diagnosis, we introduce REALHOP, a diagnose-construct-verify framework that rebinds entities, factorizes selected relations, adds complete competing paths, and places evidence at traceable locations. Structural and semantic checks precede freezing; behavioral interventions follow. On 790 paired MuSiQue questions, REALHOP raises panel-mean BNR from 27.4% to 94.4% while retaining high Full accuracy. It also yields high BNR on REALHOP-FRAMES and REALHOP-LONGBENCH. On 216 long-context questions, the matched multiple-choice spread across 16 models grows from 13.9 to 59.2 points and persists under repeated open-ended evaluation. Together, these results show that a conceptually coherent chain and a correct final answer do not by themselves establish multi-hop reasoning. Verifying that success depends on every intended hop is therefore as fundamental to multi-hop evaluation as measuring answer accuracy itself.

</details>


### [108] [CF-LoRA: Decoupled Factor Aggregation and Adaptation-Aware Client Clustering for Federated LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36986)
*Mengjun Yi,Langxing Yang,Suhan Guo,Furao Shen,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CF-LoRA框架，通过解耦LoRA因子聚合与基于适应模式的客户端聚类，解决联邦学习中小样本异构数据导致的结构失配和统计协作失配问题，在保持低秩结构的同时显著提升多模态任务的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Federated LoRA微调在处理异构客户端数据时存在两个根本性失配：一是因独立平均LoRA因子导致的结构聚合失配；二是因对不同客户端强制使用单一全局适配器而导致的统计协作失配。

Method: 提出CF-LoRA框架，包含三个核心步骤：1）学习全局共享的A因子并保留个性化的Bi因子；2）根据学习到的Bi因子的余弦相似度识别具有相似适应模式的客户端进行聚类；3）在冻结A因子的前提下，执行簇内B因子聚合。

Result: 在RoBERTa（4项语言任务）和ViT（4项视觉数据集）上的实验表明，CF-LoRA实现了两种模态下最高的平均准确率，且每个优化轮次仅需通信一个LoRA因子，兼顾了性能与效率。

Conclusion: CF-LoRA通过解耦LoRA因子聚合和引入适应感知聚类，有效缓解了联邦微调中的结构和统计失配问题，证明了该方法在多模态场景下的优越性和高效性。

Abstract: Federated LoRA fine-tuning enables parameter-efficient adaptation of pre-trained models without sharing private data, but suffers from two fundamental mismatches under heterogeneous client data: a structural aggregation mismatch caused by independently averaging LoRA factors, and a statistical collaboration mismatch caused by enforcing a single global adapter across divergent clients. To address these issues, we propose CF-LoRA, a clustered federated LoRA fine-tuning framework that combines decoupled factor aggregation with adaptation-aware client clustering. CF-LoRA first learns a globally shared $A$ factor while retaining personalized $B_i$ factors, then identifies clients with similar adaptation patterns based on the cosine similarity of their learned $B_i$ factors, and finally performs intra-cluster $B$-factor aggregation with a frozen $A$ factor. By decoupling LoRA factor aggregation, CF-LoRA preserves the low-rank structure and mitigates the structural aggregation mismatch, while adaptation-aware clustering promotes collaboration among clients with similar adaptation patterns and reduces negative transfer caused by statistical heterogeneity. Experiments on four language tasks and four vision datasets with RoBERTa and ViT show that CF-LoRA achieves the highest average accuracy in both modalities while communicating only one LoRA factor per optimization round.

</details>


### [109] [ImbalancE: Inference-Time Latent Search Against Degree Imbalance in Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36996)
*Alberto Bernardi,Luca Costabello,Christophe Gueret*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文指出知识图谱嵌入（KGE）模型在处理实体度严重不平衡的测试三元组时表现不佳，并提出了一种在推理阶段进行潜在搜索优化的方法，通过探索预训练模型的嵌入空间并融合已知与带外信息，显著改善了此类极端不平衡案例的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明KGE模型在不同区域学到的表示质量差异大，传统研究多将此归因于关系类型或度数偏差。本文旨在揭示更广泛的问题根源：预测结果与测试三元组中目标实体和锚点实体的度数不平衡程度密切相关，特别是当目标实体度数远小于锚点实体时，预测极为困难。这一问题在推荐系统等场景中至关重要，因为这些情况代表了关键的边缘案例（corner cases）。

Method: 提出了一种推理时的潜在搜索优化方法（inference-time latent search optimization method）。该方法构建在预训练模型之上，在评估/推理阶段探索嵌入空间，通过混合已知信息和带外信息（out-of-band information）来缓解度数不平衡带来的偏差，从而优化对最不平衡三元组的预测。

Result: 在包含常见基准数据集的不平衡三元组上验证了该方法的有效性，实验结果显示其性能优于常规方法，证明了其在改善关键边缘案例预测方面的价值。

Conclusion: 该研究解决了知识图谱嵌入模型在实体度数极度不平衡场景下的性能瓶颈，为KGE模型在推荐系统等依赖处理此类边缘案例的实际应用中的成功部署铺平了道路。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph Embedding models have been extensively used to learn representations of entities and relations in Knowledge Graphs for predicting missing links. However, the quality of the learned representations varies a lot across different areas of the graph. If previous research has loosely linked the problem to relation types or degree bias, we show that it is more widespread and it correlates with the degree imbalance of the entities in test triples. In particular, the prediction of a target entity that has a degree much smaller than the degree of the anchor entity is extremely problematic. This is critical in recommender systems and other use cases, where these triples represent important corner cases. To address this issue, we propose an inference-time latent search optimization method capable of significantly improving model predictions on the most imbalanced triples. Built on top of a pre-trained model, it explores the embedding space at evaluation time, blending known and out-of-band information to mitigate the degree imbalance bias. We show the value of our approach on imbalanced triples from common benchmark datasets, where we outperform conventional methods, opening the door to the successful adoption of Knowledge Graph Embedding models on these critical corner cases.

</details>


### [110] [CADOC: Cache-Aware Dynamic Object Context for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37012)
*Junjie Yao,Zhangchen Zhou,Zhi-Qin John Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CADOC算法，通过动态批量替换结构化对象为紧凑检索卡片，在保持缓存复用优势的同时显著降低长 horizon 智能体的上下文管理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长 horizon 智能体面临的上下文瓶颈问题：历史过长导致窗口限制、推理能力下降，且频繁重发历史造成高成本；现有方法难以兼顾提示词压缩与前缀缓存（prefix-cache）复用效率。

Method: 提出 CADOC (Cache-Aware Dynamic Object Context) 在线算法，将结构化对象替换为紧凑的检索 Cards 以缩短 prompt，同时保留原始内容的按需检索能力。该算法基于经济订货量（EOQ）权衡模型，通过平衡累积等待成本与共享缓存重建成本来制定动态批量调度规则。

Result: 在评估中，CADOC 在所有对比配置中实现了最低的聚合输入成本，平均降低约 40% 的输入成本，同时保持了接近完整上下文的任务性能。

Conclusion: 高效的上下文压缩不仅依赖于缩短 prompt，还取决于通过优化编辑调度来保留缓存复用机制；CADOC 提供了一条通过成本推导实现可压缩上下文管理的有效路径。

Abstract: For a long-horizon agent, context is the bottleneck: the history is resent with every request, the window caps task length, and reasoning degrades as the history grows. Replacing structured objects with compact retrieval Cards shortens the prompt and keeps the exact originals retrievable, but editing the history can break prefix-cache reuse, and prior recoverable methods time their edits by forecasts of future reuse or by preset intervals. We propose CADOC (Cache-Aware Dynamic Object Context), an online algorithm that replaces structured objects with compact Cards while preserving exact, on-demand retrieval of their original contents. CADOC schedules replacements in batches by balancing accumulated waiting cost against shared cache-reconstruction cost. Its scheduling rule follows from an economic order quantity trade-off, recovers the optimal integer batch under stationary assumptions. Across evaluation, CADOC consistently achieves the lowest aggregate input cost among the compared configurations, which reduces input cost by approximately 40\% on average while maintaining task performance close to full context. CADOC thus provides a cost-derived approach to compressible context management, demonstrating that efficient compression depends not only on shortening prompts but also on scheduling edits to preserve cache reuse.

</details>


### [111] [Physics-Informed Multi-Agent Coordination for Hospital Patient Flow Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37022)
*Guoqing Zhang,Rafik Hadfi,Takayuki Ito*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种名为“物理信息多智能体协调”（Physics-Informed Multi-Agent Coordination）的框架，将经典BCMP排队网络作为物理先验嵌入到去中心化多智能体强化学习中，以解决医院部门间患者流协调难题。该方法在MIMIC-IV真实数据上验证，显著降低了系统总延迟并保证了临床安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于开放BCMP网络的队列理论模型依赖平稳假设和固定路由矩阵，难以适应具有状态依赖性的现实动态；而集中式强化学习方法无法适应医院治理的去中心化结构（各临床部门拥有局部观测、异质资源和不同目标）。因此，需要一种既能利用排队论解释性，又能处理去中心化决策和多agent协作的新方法。

Method: 构建了基于Dec-POMDP的多智能体框架，将经验校准的BCMP排队拓扑作为物理先验嵌入去中心化多智能体强化学习架构中。具体机制包括：1）自主部门智能体合作协商患者路由和动态服务扩展；2）通过在网络边缘交换局部动作指纹来缓解环境非平稳性，避免过高通信开销；3）优化空间分解奖励结构。

Result: 基于MIMIC-IV真实患者轨迹的实验评估显示，该合作多智能体方法相比静态马尔可夫近似、启发式调度以及独立多智能体基线，大幅减少了累积系统延迟，同时维持了临床安全约束。

Conclusion: 结合物理先验（BCMP排队网络）与去中心化多智能体强化学习是解决医院复杂患者流协调的有效途径，能在保证去中心化治理结构和临床安全的前提下，显著提升资源利用效率和降低等待时间。

Abstract: Efficient patient flow coordination across autonomous hospital departments is critical for mitigating overcrowding and balancing resource utilization. While classical queueing theory, specifically open Baskett--Chandy--Muntz--Palacios (BCMP) networks, provides an interpretable mathematical topology for healthcare operations, analytical models rely on stationary assumptions and fixed routing matrices that degrade under state-dependent real-world dynamics. Conversely, centralized reinforcement learning approaches struggle to accommodate the decentralized structure of hospital governance, where individual clinical departments function with localized observations, heterogeneous resources, and divergent operational objectives. In this paper, we present a Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) framework titled \emph{Physics-Informed Multi-Agent Coordination}, which embeds empirically calibrated BCMP queueing topologies as physical priors within a decentralized multi-agent reinforcement learning architecture. Formulated as a Decentralized Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (Dec-POMDP) under coupled resource constraints, our method enables autonomous departmental agents to cooperatively negotiate patient routing and dynamic service scaling. To mitigate environmental non-stationarity without inducing excessive communication overhead, agents exchange localized action fingerprints along network edges and optimize a spatially decomposed reward structure. Empirical evaluations driven by real-world MIMIC-IV patient trajectories indicate that this cooperative multi-agent approach substantially reduces cumulative system delay compared to static Markovian approximations, heuristic dispatching, and independent multi-agent baselines, while maintaining clinical safety constraints.

</details>


### [112] [Language as the Interface: Foundation-Model Contrastive Learning Links Transcriptomes and Electrophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37024)
*Junbo Shen,Jinying Gao,Bo Lei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出LangPatch，一种基于对比学习的多模态基础模型框架，通过语言接口将转录组数据（GenePT）与电生理表型对齐，显著提升了跨模态预测精度及物种/脑区间的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建神经科学多模态基础模型需要整合转录组学和电生理学数据；现有方法在跨模态对齐、预测精度及模型泛化性方面存在不足。

Method: 利用Patch-seq配对数据，使用冻结的文本编码器嵌入基因描述和言语化的电生理特征，通过上下文适配器连接模态，并采用成对对比学习框架训练LangPatch模型。

Result: 在小鼠视觉/运动皮层及人类大脑皮层队列中，LangPatch实现了最高的转录组-电生理预测相关性，优于基线方法如JAMIE；成功预测转录组家族、类型、皮层层级及标记基因表达；具备跨脑区和跨物种迁移能力（小鼠运动皮层约70%保持率，人类皮层47%-58%保持率）。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了神经元分子表示与功能表示的有效对齐，为构建神经科学领域的多模态基础模型提供了关键组件。

Abstract: Integrating transcriptomic and electrophysiological data is essential for building multimodal foundation models for neuroscience. Patch-seq provides paired measurements of gene expression and intrinsic electrophysiology from the same neuron, establishing a basis for training cross-modal models. Here we introduce LangPatch, a foundation-model-based contrastive learning framework that uses paired Patch-seq data to align pretrained GenePT representations with electrophysiological phenotypes through a language-based interface. Gene descriptions and verbalized electrophysiological profiles are embedded by the same frozen text encoder. A context adapter and projection modules connect the modalities through paired contrastive learning. Across mouse visual, mouse motor, and human cortical cohorts, LangPatch achieves the highest mean transcriptome-to-electrophysiology prediction correlation among the evaluated foundation-model and representation-learning methods. It also improves held-out cross-modal alignment in the two mouse cohorts (FOSCTTM 0.107/0.135 vs. 0.208/0.222 for JAMIE, an existing cross-modal Patch-seq imputation method). It predicts transcriptomic family, type, cortical layer, and marker-gene expression from electrophysiology, exceeding other baselines on most endpoints. More importantly, the method transfers across brain areas and species: a model trained on mouse visual cortex predicts electrophysiology in motor cortex with approximately 70% correlation retention and in human cortex with 47% (58% on acute-slice recordings). Together, these results demonstrate alignment between molecular and functional representations of neurons, providing a building block for multimodal foundation models in neuroscience.

</details>


### [113] [AnyAct: Universal Action for Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37025)
*Lingrui Xu,Yangqin Jiang,Jiachang Zhang,Xubin Ren,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 AnyAct，一个统一的行动层，通过分层检索和异质性反馈统一解决大语言模型智能体在大规模动态工具生态中的“规模困境”、“非平稳性”和“异构性”挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决AI智能体在开放世界复杂任务中面临的三个核心挑战：1. 工具生态系统庞大超出LLM上下文窗口（规模困境）；2. 工具质量因更新或故障而波动（非平稳性）；3. 多模态反馈格式不同导致信息孤岛（异构性）。

Method: 1. 构建自进化行动空间：通过分层渐进检索过滤相关行动，通过测试时可靠性演化修剪不可靠行动。2. 引入异质性观察接地模块统一多模态反馈。3. 定义混合行动空间（基础+语义行动），优化成功率与执行成本的平衡。4. 开发OSMCP新基准。

Result: 在LiveMCPBench和OSMCP基准上达到最先进性能。显著优于基线方法，尤其对原生能力受限的模型效果明显。在OSMCP上以仅50步实现77.27%的整体成功率，步骤数仅为竞争对手的一半。

Conclusion: AnyAct成功实现了智能体在大规模、动态工具生态中的高效可靠操作，证明了其在处理复杂序列任务方面的优越性，特别是在提升执行效率方面具有显著优势。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) advance, AI agents are increasingly deployed in open-world environments to tackle complex sequential tasks (e.g., document processing, cross-application collaboration), relying heavily on actions ranging from GUI operations to semantic APIs. However, three core challenges persist: the "scale dilemma" of massive tool ecosystems exceeding LLM context windows, the "non-stationarity" of tool quality due to updates or outages, and the "heterogeneity" of feedback formats (pixels, text, structured data) creating information silos. To address these, we propose AnyAct, a universal action layer that unifies available capabilities into a self-evolving action space, enabling agents to operate efficiently and reliably in large-scale, dynamic tool ecosystems. AnyAct's core design focuses on two objectives: constructing this action space via hierarchical progressive retrieval (filtering task-relevant actions) and test-time reliability evolution (pruning unreliable actions), and enabling reliability-aware action orchestration through a heterogeneous observation grounding module that unifies multi-modal feedback. Additionally, it defines a hybrid action space (primitive + semantic actions) and optimizes for a balance between task success rate and execution cost. Evaluations on LiveMCPBench and OSMCP (a new benchmark we developed for multi-granularity action collaboration) demonstrate state-of-the-art performance. AnyAct delivers substantial performance gains over baseline methods across various LLM base models on LiveMCPBench and improvements are particularly notable for models with constrained native capabilities. On OSMCP, it achieves 77.27% overall success with only 50 steps, which is half the steps required by most competitors.

</details>


### [114] [Beyond Low-Rank Parameterization: Narrowing the Gap Between LoRA and Full Fine-Tuning via Gradient Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37027)
*Yihao Ouyang,Shiwei Li,Haozhao Wang,Xiandi Luo,Zhuoqi Hu,Jinglun Yu,Yichen Li,Ruixuan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出GDLoRA，通过梯度分解提取法向分量直接更新基座权重，在保持LoRA内存优势的同时缩小与全量微调的性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决低秩适应（LoRA）在全量微调（FFT）面前存在的性能差距问题，突破现有LoRA变体受限于当前参数化的梯度空间约束。

Method: 对当前模型参数处的全量权重重力进行正交分解，定义垂直于LoRA可访问梯度空间的分量为“法向梯度”；通过前向激活和反向信号重构全重力并提取该法向分量，直接用于更新基座权重，同时保留标准AdamW优化LoRA因子。

Result: 在自然语言理解、数学推理、常识推理和图像分类任务上，GDLoRA一致性地优于LoRA，有效缩小了与全量微调的性能差距，且未增加标准的优化器状态内存开销。

Conclusion: 引入法向梯度的梯度分解策略能显著提升参数高效微调效果，证明了利用额外梯度信息更新基座权重的有效性，为PEFT提供了新的优化视角。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a widely used approach to parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT), yet a performance gap can remain relative to full fine-tuning (FFT). Many LoRA variants improve the initialization or optimization of low-rank factors. At each training step, however, their first-order weight-space directions are constrained by the current parameterization. We characterize the corresponding LoRA-accessible gradient space and show that it coincides with the tangent space induced by the current LoRA parameterization. This characterization yields an orthogonal decomposition of the full weight gradient at the current model parameters. We term the component orthogonal to this space the normal gradient. Based on this decomposition, we propose GDLoRA (Gradient-Decomposed Low-Rank Adaptation). GDLoRA reconstructs the full weight gradient from forward activations and backward signals, extracts its normal component, and directly updates the base weights with this component, while retaining standard AdamW optimization for the LoRA factors. GDLoRA incorporates complementary normal gradients without increasing standard LoRA's optimizer-state memory budget under matched adapter and optimizer configurations. Experiments on natural language understanding, mathematical reasoning, commonsense reasoning, and image classification show that GDLoRA consistently improves over LoRA and narrows the performance gap to FFT. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/GDLoRA.

</details>


### [115] [FedLAFP: Low-Rank Aggregation Meets Full-Rank Personalization in Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37033)
*Mengjun Yi,Huaian Gu,Yinghao Ai,Furao Shen,Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FedLAFP框架，通过结合全局聚合的LoRA共享分支和客户端私有的RandLoRA私有分支，解决联邦学习中数据异构导致的个性化预测问题，在无需额外通信的情况下提升个人化准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化联邦微调方法通常对共享和私人适配使用相同的低秩结构，忽视了它们在知识聚合与个性化适应方面的不同需求，导致难以有效平衡共性知识的转移与个性特征的学习。

Method: 构建角色感知的双分支框架：1) 紧凑的全局聚合LoRA分支用于高效传输通用知识；2) 客户端私有的RandLoRA分支，利用固定的随机低秩基和可学习的缩放系数进行 expressive 的客户端特定适配；3) 通过客户端和层级特定的混合系数融合两分支，仅交换共享LoRA参数。

Result: 在四个视觉识别基准测试中，FedLAFP一致优于仅本地训练和联邦LoRA基线，平均个人化准确率达到86.93%，比最佳基线高出1.30个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验支持该角色分配策略：LoRA能产生更对齐的客户端更新并降低聚合误差，而RandLoRA更准确地恢复客户端特异性残差，证明了该架构在联邦个性化微调中的有效性。

Abstract: Federated parameter-efficient fine-tuning enables clients to adapt pre-trained models without sharing raw data or communicating the full model, but statistical heterogeneity makes a single global adapter insufficient for personalized prediction. Existing personalized methods typically use the same low-rank structure for both shared and private adaptation, overlooking their distinct requirements for aggregation and personalization. We propose FedLAFP, a role-aware framework that couples a compact, globally aggregated LoRA branch with a client-private, full-rank-capable RandLoRA branch. The shared branch provides an efficient interface for transferring common knowledge, whereas the private branch combines fixed random low-rank bases with learned scaling coefficients to provide expressive client-specific adaptation without additional communication. Client- and layer-specific mixing coefficients jointly fuse the two branches, and only the shared LoRA parameters are exchanged. A controlled linear study supports this role assignment: LoRA yields more aligned client updates and lower aggregation error, while RandLoRA more accurately recovers client-specific residuals. Experiments across four visual recognition benchmarks show that FedLAFP consistently outperforms local-only and federated LoRA baselines, achieving an average personalized accuracy of $86.93\%$ and exceeding the best baseline average by $1.30$ percentage points.

</details>


### [116] [Watch-Think-Interact: Bootstrapping Long-Horizon Multi-Turn Streaming Video Reasoning with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37035)
*Ziheng Huang,Yicheng Bao,Xueheng Li,Zhenkun Gao,Bangwei Liu,Kunquan Li,Yuxiang Shen,Bangyan Li,Xuejiao Wang,Changbo Wang,Gaoqi He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Watch-Think-Interact (WTI) 闭环框架，通过紧凑的自然语言记忆和选择性视频回溯解决流式视频推理中的异步多问题上下文限制与细节遗漏问题，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理固定上下文预算下的异步多问题时，预先形成的在线状态可能因后续问题的揭示而遗漏关键视觉细节，且无法仅凭保留的状态恢复这些细节。

Method: 1. 构建WTI闭环框架：维护带有时间范围的紧凑自然语言记忆条目；根据当前上下文和记忆充足性，决定直接回答、继续观看或选择性回溯过往视频片段进行再决策。2. 构建WTI-82K数据集：包含4,812条因果对齐轨迹的82,335个定时问题。3. 开发Stream-GDPO算法：利用轨迹级反馈优化响应时机、视频回溯和记忆更新。

Result: 在开源流式基线中取得最先进水平，StreamingBench准确率达83.3%，OVO-Bench加权总体准确率达到73.6%。

Conclusion: WTI框架通过动态的记忆管理和选择性视频回溯机制，有效解决了流式视频推理中的信息丢失问题，显著提升了多问题场景下的推理精度。

Abstract: Streaming video assistance requires models to answer asynchronous questions from an observed prefix under a fixed context budget. Existing approaches model response timing or compress history, but an online state formed before future questions are known can omit visual details before later questions reveal their relevance; the retained state alone cannot recover them. We introduce Watch-Think-Interact (WTI), a closed-loop framework for multi-question streaming video reasoning. WTI maintains compact natural-language memory entries tagged with source-video time ranges; these entries support direct reasoning when sufficient and otherwise anchor selective recall of finer visual evidence. For each question, WTI answers when current context and memory suffice, continues watching when required evidence has not appeared, or recalls a relevant past interval and decides again after incorporating the returned chunks, without replaying the full observed history. To train this behavior, we construct WTI-82K, comprising 82,335 timed questions across 4,812 causally aligned trajectories, and develop Stream-GDPO to optimize complete multi-question streaming rollouts using trajectory-level feedback for response timing, source-video recall, and memory updates. WTI achieves state-of-the-art aggregate performance among the compared open-source streaming baselines, reaching 83.3% on StreamingBench and 73.6% weighted overall accuracy on OVO-Bench.

</details>


### [117] [MatToolBench: Benchmarking Multimodal Agents in Real-World Materials Science Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37053)
*Mei Wu,Rui Xie,Runyu Zhang,Yuqiang Li,Tianfan Fu,Bo Chen,Kai Yu,Xin Chen,Lu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出首个真实环境下的材料科学软件多模态GUI代理基准测试MatToolBench，揭示通用基准高性能模型在专业科学工作流中存在显著能力差距，失败源于领域知识缺失及跨工具协作薄弱而非单纯的视觉定位问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管多模态GUI代理在通用软件基准上表现优异，但其在专业科学软件（如材料科学）中的应用尚未被充分探索。由于领域数据稀疏、界面专业化及隐式工作流惯例，通用预训练无法有效覆盖此类场景。

Method: 构建包含10种工具、204个任务的MatToolBench基准，涵盖GUI操作、OriginPro脚本及代码数据库查询三种模式，均在Windows 11虚拟机中执行。通过领域专家细粒度分解任务标准实现可解释的部分评分，并引入人工-LLM一致性研究验证多模态评判者用于美学评估的有效性。

Result: 实验显示通用基准的强势表现无法迁移至专业科学工作流；最佳模型在GUI任务中的成功率仅达25%，代码任务为45%。分析指出失败根源包括领域特定操作知识缺乏、科学软件预训练覆盖率低、跨工具工件传递能力弱以及关键状态仅以视觉形式暴露。

Conclusion: MatToolBench作为一个具有挑战性的诊断性基准和真实环境测试平台，为数据稀缺、知识密集型的工作流提供了重要评估依据，证明了当前模型在处理专业科学软件时的局限性。

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have achieved impressive results on general software benchmarks, yet their ability to operate professional scientific software remains largely unexplored. In materials science, sparse domain-specific web data, specialized interfaces, and tacit workflow conventions create blind spots that general-purpose pretraining cannot readily bridge. We present MatToolBench, the first real-environment benchmark for evaluating multimodal GUI agents on professional materials science software, comprising 204 tasks across 10 tools in three modalities: GUI operation, OriginPro scripting, and code-based database queries, all executed inside a Windows 11 VM. Each task is decomposed into fine-grained sub-criteria by domain experts, enabling interpretable partial-credit scoring; the GUI component of our multi-level evaluation pipeline achieves an average F1 of 0.98. For OriginPro figure-generation tasks, we further conduct a human-LLM agreement study to validate the use of a multimodal judge for secondary aesthetic assessment. Our experiments show that strong performance on general benchmarks does not transfer to professional scientific workflows, and that this gap is not a visual-grounding problem alone: failures arise from domain-specific operational knowledge, sparse pretraining coverage of scientific software, weak cross-tool artifact handoff, and critical states exposed only visually. Even the best model reaches only 25% success rate on GUI tasks and 45% on code tasks. MatToolBench therefore serves as a challenging diagnostic benchmark and real-environment testbed for data-scarce, knowledge-intensive scientific workflows.

</details>


### [118] [actr: aligning thoughts and responses for multilingual safety in reasoning llms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37054)
*Xianhui Zhang,Jian Yu,Chengyu Xie,Chenhang Cui,Shuyi Miao,Pengyang Shao,Yu Zheng,Fei Shen,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ACTR框架，通过跨语言思维与响应对齐增强大语言模型在多语言环境下的安全对齐，解决非高资源语言下越狱攻击的安全漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确保跨语言推理大型语言模型（LLMs）的安全性对于其可靠部署至关重要，但在非高资源语言中，模型即使识别出安全风险仍可能生成不安全响应。

Method: 提出跨语言思维与响应对齐（ACTR）框架：1) 定义思维差距分数（TGS）和推理轨迹替换以衡量跨语言安全差距；2) 通过神经元掩码评估重要性并识别支持安全推理的“安全思维神经元”；3) 设计神经元选择性一致性优化（NSCO），在冻结评判模型奖励推理与响应一致性的同时，仅更新选中神经元的参数。

Result: 在两个推理模型上，ACTR在AdvBench-X和MultiJail数据集上的平均攻击成功率低于现有最先进方法，且安全增益延伸至未见过的语言，同时保持或提升了多语言知识和数学推理任务的性能，并限制了良性请求的错误拒绝。

Conclusion: ACTR框架能有效提升多语言大语言模型的安全对齐能力，通过增强现有安全推理的使用，实现了更强的跨语言防御效果而不损害通用性能。

Abstract: Ensuring the safety of reasoning large language models (LLMs) across languages is essential for their reliable deployment. However, when exposed to jailbreak attacks in non-high-resource languages, these models may generate unsafe responses even when their reasoning traces identify safety risks. To address this issue, we propose aligning cross-lingual thoughts and responses (ACTR), a framework that improves multilingual safety alignment by strengthening the use of existing safety reasoning. Specifically, we first present the think gap score (TGS) to compare the normalized contributions of reasoning traces to attention outputs during response generation across languages, and use reasoning- trace substitution to measure the cross-lingual safety gap. Next, using a corpus of jailbreak queries, we assess neuron importance through changes in response representations caused by neuron masking and compare the high-importance neuron sets obtained with reasoning enabled and disabled to identify safety think neurons that support the use of safety reasoning. Finally, we devise neuron-selective consistency optimization (NSCO), which uses a frozen judge model to reward agreement between the safety categories of reasoning traces and responses while updating only the parameters associated with the selected neurons, requiring no human-annotated responses or preference data. Across two reasoning models, ACTR achieves lower average attack success rates than the evaluated state-of-the-art methods on AdvBench-X and MultiJail, with safety gains extending to unseen languages, while preserving or improving average performance on multilingual knowledge and mathematical reasoning tasks and limiting false refusals of benign requests. Warning: this paper contains examples with unsafe content.

</details>


### [119] [Breaking the Illusion of Review Reliability under Static Evaluation: SCOPE Fuzzing for LLM-based Scientific Reviewers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37097)
*Zhuo Chen,Hao Zeng,Jiawei Liu,Guoxiu He,Le Cai,Liu Haotan,Li Wenbo,Yong Huang,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究构建了三层次扰动评估框架，发现基于静态模板的LLM审查存在分层脆弱性和覆盖不足问题。为此提出SCOPE-Fuzzer策略感知模糊测试方法，通过动态自适应突变有效揭示了先前被忽视的审查漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管前期研究表明LLM审查者具有一定的可靠性（如对过度宣称的惩罚），但这些结论主要基于使用静态模板实现的狭窄扰动策略集，缺乏对实际可靠性的充分证据支持。现有静态评估无法全面反映LLM在面对多样化、真实场景扰动时的脆弱性。

Method: 1. 构建涵盖表面呈现、论证逻辑和价值判断三个层次的扰动评估框架；
2. 对代表性LLM-based reviewers进行实验，识别静态评估的两项局限：分层脆弱性（扰动效果依赖原稿评分高低）和扰动覆盖不足（单一模板遗漏多样实现暴露的漏洞）；
3. 提出SCOPE-Fuzzer：一种结合反馈驱动策略选择与内容自适应突变的策略感知模糊测试器，通过迭代探测动态扰动来持续揭示审查者的潜在漏洞。

Result: SCOPE-Fuzzer在实验中被证实能一致地发现静态评估及其他基线方法所忽略的LLM审查者脆弱性，证明了动态扰动在全面评估LLM审查可靠性方面的有效性。

Conclusion: LLM-based审稿系统在当前静态评估下存在系统性评估盲区，其可靠性受原始评分分层影响且面临多种未覆盖的扰动风险。采用策略感知的动态模糊测试方法（如SCOPE-Fuzzer）是更有效地验证和提升LLM审稿鲁棒性与安全性的必要途径。

Abstract: The rapid growth of submissions and reviewing workload has accelerated the use of large language models (LLMs) in peer review. Prior studies suggest that LLM-based reviewers can penalize content perturbations, such as overclaiming, indicating a certain degree of reliability. Yet these conclusions are largely based on a narrow set of perturbation strategies instantiated with static templates, providing limited evidence of actual reliability. In this paper, we construct a three-level evaluation framework covering perturbations to surface presentation, argumentative logic, and value judgment. Experiments on representative LLM-based reviewers reveal two limitations of static evaluation: stratified vulnerability, where perturbation effects depend on whether the paper's original review score is high or low, and perturbation undercoverage, where a single template misses vulnerabilities exposed by diverse realizations. To address these limitations, we propose SCOPE-Fuzzer, a strategy-aware fuzzer that combines feedback-driven strategy selection with adaptive mutation of paper content. By iteratively probing reviewers with dynamic perturbations, SCOPE-Fuzzer consistently uncovers vulnerabilities overlooked by static evaluation and other baselines.

</details>


### [120] [Learning from Viable Failure Prefixes: Milestone Viability Potential Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37111)
*Qi Zhou,Yuanfan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MVPO算法，通过估计前缀潜力和修复零信用组，解决长视界LLM智能体在稀疏奖励下中间决策归因失败的问题，显著提升ALFWorld和WebShop任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于群体的强化学习方法（如GRPO、GiGPO）在比较返回结果无差异时失效，导致早期训练中包含有用前缀的失败轨迹无法获得具有任务区分度的优势分配，称为“零信用失效”。

Method: 提出了里程碑可行性势策略优化（MVPO），一种基于潜在路由的策略优化算法。具体包括：在并查集可行性区域内估算前缀潜力；利用势差优势修复零信用组；根据相对性能进展衰减势分支。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct基准测试中，MVPO优于8个强基线。在相同训练长度下，相比GiGPO在ALFWorld上提升4.4个百分点的成功率，在WebShop上提升5.3个百分点，且仅增加0.16%-0.20%的优势构建开销。

Conclusion: MVPO有效解决了长期视界智能体训练中的零信用归因问题，以极小的计算开销实现了显著的性能提升，证明了利用可行性失败前缀进行有效学习的重要性。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require reinforcement learning methods that can assign credit to intermediate decisions under sparse and delayed rewards. Existing group-based methods such as GRPO and GiGPO alleviate this issue by comparing rollout returns or repeated anchor states, but they still fail when the compared returns have no variation. We identify this failure mode as zero-credit failure: during early training, many failed rollouts contain useful prefixes, yet existing methods assign them no task-discriminative advantage. To address this issue, we propose Milestone Viability Potential Policy Optimization (MVPO), a potential-routed policy optimization algorithm that learns from viable failure prefixes. MVPO estimates prefix potential over Union-Find viability regions, repairs zero-credit groups with potential-difference advantages, and attenuates the potential branch according to relative performance progress. Experiments with Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct show that MVPO outperforms eight strong baselines, including GRPO and GiGPO. Under the same training length, MVPO improves over the GiGPO baseline by +4.4 success points on ALFWorld and +5.3 on WebShop, while adding only 0.16%-0.20% advantage-construction overhead.

</details>


### [121] [When Should Agents Check External State? Budgeting Observations for Stored Intentions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37125)
*Zhengkun Di,Bin Shi,Kai Sun,Yiming Xu,Bo Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出BudgetPM，一种针对前瞻性记忆外部观察的共享预算资源分配框架。通过静态（Logistic打分）和序列（蒸馏 hindsight 策略）两种变体，在严格预算下显著减少观测成本（42-54%），同时保持高质量表现或超越现有最强监测调度方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决前瞻性记忆中存储的意图与当前条件检查之间的冲突问题。现有系统虽能判断意图是否需要关注，但缺乏在共享Episode预算下合理分配观测资源的能力，导致成本效率低下或资源浪费。

Method: 引入首次针对外部观察资源的分配 formulation。包含两个共享硬预算执行器的策略变体：1) BudgetPM-Static：使用轻量级Logistic分类器学习单次检查是否改善当前决策；2) BudgetPM-Sequential：通过蒸馏全Episode后验时间表，利用仅依赖查询前信息的轻量级策略动态决定消耗或保留容量。

Result: 1) 在两组基准测试和三组基础模型上，BudgetPM-Static优于Mem0和PMA工作流；Logistic分类器在相同质量-成本权衡下表现竞争力，且用42-54%更少的观测数保留99.9-100%无约束质量。2) 严重稀缺场景下，BudgetPM-Sequential比最强自然监测调度Set F1高1.92-2.58分，以16-33%更少观测数实现相同性能。

Conclusion: 确立“需求-能力”设计准则：当容量覆盖需求时，局部门控有效；当跨时间竞争观测机会时，具备未来意识的监督机制增加价值。证明了在预算约束下，前瞻性记忆的监测可被优化为高效的需求响应机制。

Abstract: Prospective memory allows an agent to retain an intention tied to a future condition, but the stored intention does not reveal whether that condition currently holds. Checking it may require web access, multi-step tool use, and paid calls. Existing systems decide when intentions require attention, but do not allocate the resulting observations under a shared budget. We introduce the first resource-allocation formulation for the external observations required by stored intentions under a shared episode budget. BudgetPM offers two policy variants that share a hard-budget executor. BudgetPM-Static uses a lightweight Logistic scorer to learn whether a check improves the current decision. BudgetPM-Sequential distills full-episode hindsight schedules into a lightweight policy that decides when to spend or reserve capacity using only pre-query information at deployment. We evaluate BudgetPM against two public memory-agent systems, five matched controls, and four hand-designed monitoring or budget-adaptation rules. Across two benchmarks and three backbones, BudgetPM-Static outperforms adapted Mem0 and PMA workflows. On PM-Bench, its Logistic scorer reaches competitive quality--cost operating points alongside higher-capacity scorers and retains 99.9--100\% of unconstrained quality with 42--54\% fewer observations. Under severe scarcity and the same hard caps, BudgetPM-Sequential exceeds the strongest tested natural monitoring schedule by 1.92--2.58 Set F1 points. It reaches the same Set F1 and on-time recall with 16--33\% fewer observations. Matched attribution, exact-cost analysis, and a fixed-budget load intervention link this gain to competition between present and future opportunities. These results yield a demand--capacity design rule: local gating works when capacity covers demand, while future-aware supervision adds value when observations compete across time.

</details>


### [122] [SkillCome: Group Contrast Skill Optimization with Dual Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37128)
*Haolin Li,Feng Hong,Ang Li,Chilin Fu,Weichang Wu,Ya Zhang,Yanfeng Wang,Xiaolu Zhang,Jiangchao Yao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SkillCome方法，通过群组对比优化和双记忆系统生成多条轨迹以增强大语言模型的技能演化，解决了单轨迹信号不足和局部噪声敏感问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能演化方法通常每问仅生成单一轨迹，导致优化信号不足：难以在失败轨迹中定位具体错误行为，也难以确定成功轨迹中应纳入技能的有效动作；且依赖局部批次数据，易受噪声干扰影响优化方向。

Method: 提出基于群组对比优化与双记忆的Skill-evolution方法（SkillCome）。针对每个问题生成多条轨迹进行群组对比分析，精准识别成败轨迹间的关键行为差异以提供可靠优化信号；双记忆系统积累历史步骤证据，追踪不同组间的共享模式，从而获得更通用的优化方向，将成功经验转化为可复用技能。

Result: 在六个涵盖问答、推理和智能体任务的基准测试中进行广泛实验。SkillCome在五种不同家族和规模的大语言模型上均优于基线方法，性能提升最高达+5.69分。

Conclusion: SkillCome构建了一个系统的优化流程，通过对比分析和长期记忆积累，有效提升了大语言模型的技能演化能力和通用性能。

Abstract: Skill evolution improves the capabilities of large language models by analyzing trajectories generated under a given skill and modifying the skill accordingly. Existing approaches typically generate a single trajectory per question. However, this provides insufficient optimization signals since it requires inferring effective skill edits from a solitary path. It is difficult to pinpoint which actions caused the failure in a failed trajectory, or to determine which actions in a successful one should be incorporated into the skill. Furthermore, they rely on a local batch of trajectories for analysis, making the optimization direction susceptible to noisy evidence. To address these, we propose SkillCome, a Skill-evolution method based on group Contrast optimization with dual memory. For each question, SkillCome generates trajectories and performs group contrast analysis to precisely identify key behavioral divergences between successful and failed trajectories, offering reliable optimization signals. The dual memory system further accumulates evidence from historical steps to track patterns shared across different groups, leading to more generalized optimization directions. Together, SkillCome builds a systematic optimization process that transforms experience from observed successful trajectories into reusable skills. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks spanning question answering, reasoning, and agentic tasks demonstrate the effectiveness of our method. SkillCome consistently outperforms baselines across five models of varying families and scales, with gains up to +5.69 points.

</details>


### [123] [Train Ahead, Distill Back: Bootstrapping On-Policy Self-Distillation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37132)
*Zheng Zhang,Xinyue Tan,Lufei Li,Xinyi Zhang,Yexin Li,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出B-OPSD方法，通过利用“未来”策略状态作为教师，为当前学生提供更强、更密集的监督信号，从而提升大语言模型在数学推理任务上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) 方法受限于当前或缓慢平均的策略状态作为自教师，导致监督质量不足，无法充分利用特权信息。

Method: 引入Bootstrapped On-Policy Self-Distillation (B-OPSD)。该方法首先临时训练策略以获取一个“未来教师”，然后将学生恢复至原始策略状态，最后使用这个“未来教师”来监督重启后的学生在on-policy轨迹上的表现。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B模型的数学推理实验中，B-OPSD相比标准OPSD取得了一致性改进。特别是在rollout-privileged设置下，得分从27.50提升至41.30（4B模型），以及从48.80提升至64.44（8B模型）。

Conclusion: 未来的学习进展可以反向蒸馏到当前模型中。这一原则允许模型在保留已习得知识的同时，突破产生该优化的初始状态限制，实现自我增强。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) improves large language models by letting a self-teacher with privileged information provide dense token-level supervision on the model's own trajectories. Yet existing methods typically construct the self-teacher from the current, initial, or slowly averaged policy state, leaving the quality of supervision constrained by the teacher's ability to exploit privileged information. We ask whether the model's own optimization progress can instead be recycled into a stronger self-teacher. In this paper, we introduce Bootstrapped On-Policy Self-Distillation (B-OPSD), which temporarily trains the policy ahead to obtain a future teacher, restores the student to the original policy state, and then uses the future teacher to supervise the restarted student. The future teacher improves supervision in two complementary ways, it can generate more reliable privileged trajectories and, conditioned on them, provide more informative token-level targets along the restarted student's on-policy trajectories. Experiments on mathematical reasoning with Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B show consistent improvements over standard OPSD in both settings, including gains from 27.50 to 41.30 and from 48.80 to 64.44 in the rollout-privileged setting. Our findings point to a broader principle for self-improving models that future learning progress can be distilled backward, preserving acquired knowledge while bootstrapping beyond the optimization state that produced it.

</details>


### [124] [From Judgment Quality to Downstream Utility: Rethinking LLM-as-a-Judge for Open-Ended Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37145)
*Zheng Zhang,Lufei Li,Xinyue Tan,Yuanhao Zeng,Ziwei Shan,Yexin Li,Kan Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了LLM-as-a-Judge在开放任务评估中的判断质量与下游效用，发现二者并不总是一致，且提示协议设计与使用方式（训练时监督vs推理时增强）显著影响效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常直接将LLM判决转化为奖励信号用于策略训练，忽视内在判断质量，且仅局限于训练时的监督用途。需要更系统地研究如何 elicitation 判决以及如何利用判决以提升性能。

Method: 1. **判决获取（Elicitation）**：调整三个维度——裁决粒度（verdict granularity）、评论使用（critique usage）、评估批量（evaluation batching）。
2. **判决使用（Usage）**：除策略训练外，扩展至测试时推理，包括Best-of-N选择、Judge引导的修正（review/revision）和束搜索（beam search）。
3. **对比分析**：考察不同协议对内在判断质量和下游任务的效用。

Result: (i) 判断质量与下游效用并非总是正相关；(ii) Judge协议设计对两者均有重大影响；(iii) Judge引导能有效将推理时的计算资源转化为性能增益，但不同推理策略的收益存在差异。

Conclusion: 针对开放任务的LLM Judges需要进行多维度的评估，既要关注其内在判断质量，也要考量其在各种应用场景下的下游效用。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-Judge is increasingly used to evaluate policy responses on open-ended tasks that lack ground-truth answers. Existing work often directly converts the resulting judgments into reward signals for policy training, paying limited attention to intrinsic judgment quality and largely restricting the use of Judges to training-time supervision. We systematically investigate judgment quality and downstream utility by examining both how judgments are elicited and how they are used. For judgment elicitation, we vary the Judge protocol along three dimensions: verdict granularity, critique usage, and evaluation batching. For judgment usage, beyond policy training, we extend Judge to test-time inference through Best-of-N selection, Judge-guided revision, and beam search. We find that, (i) Surprisingly, judgment quality and downstream utility do not always align. (ii) Judge protocol design substantially affects both intrinsic judgment quality and downstream utility. (iii) Judge guidance effectively converts test-time compute into performance gains, with benefits varying across inference strategies. Our results call for a multifaceted evaluation of LLM Judges on open-ended tasks, encompassing intrinsic judgment quality, and downstream utility.

</details>


### [125] [When Tools Silently Lie: Evaluating and Mitigating Blind Compliance in Tool-Augmented Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37153)
*Zifu Tao,Changqing Yin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍了ToxicBench，用于评估工具增强型数据智能体在面对各种错误证据时的信任与验证行为。研究发现 poisoned 观察会显著降低任务成功率（26-39%），重试机制在单一中毒下有效，但在重复中毒下智能体仍会在检查后采纳错误答案。智能体评分器与人类标注有96%的一致性，强调了证据可用性和答案选择是衡量智能体可靠性的两个互补维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具增强型智能体依赖工具输出进行分析决策，但成功的执行可能返回看似合理但不正确的证据。需要理解智能体是否信任或验证这些证据，因此需要同时考察通过检查获得的证据和最终采纳的答案。

Method: 引入ToxicBench基准测试，通过在固定源数据上配对清洁和中毒观测值，测量在数值、标签、模式和检索错误下的检查和采纳行为。在118个任务上使用GPT配合三个适配器进行评估。进行公开表格的控制实验以隔离提供证据对恢复的影响。冻结评分器后，将其对200条轨迹的判断与人类注释进行比较。

Result: 在所有三个适配器的118个任务的GPT评估中，中毒将任务成功率降低了26至39个百分点。单次中毒情况下，普通重试有所帮助，但重复中毒揭示了检查后错误答案的采纳。在三个公共表格上的控制实验隔离了提供证据对恢复的影响。冻结后的评分器与200条轨迹的人类注释在任务成功方面达到了96%的一致性。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显了证据可用性和答案选择作为智能体可靠性的互补维度。发布的数据轨迹、版本化评分以及参考和交付审计为后续研究提供了资源，表明即使经过检查，智能体也可能采纳错误答案，且重试机制的效果取决于中毒类型。

Abstract: Tool-augmented data agents rely on tool outputs for analytical decisions. Yet successful execution can return plausible but incorrect evidence, requiring agents to decide whether to trust or verify it. Understanding this failure requires examining both the evidence obtained through checking and the answer ultimately adopted. We introduce ToxicBench to measure checking and adoption under numerical, label, schema, and retrieval errors, pairing clean and poisoned observations over fixed source data. In the 118-task GPT evaluation across three adapters, poisoning lowers task success by 26 to 39 percentage points. Ordinary retries help under one-shot poisoning, whereas repeated poisoning reveals wrong-answer adoption after checking. Controls on three public tables isolate how supplied evidence affects recovery. After freezing the scorer, we compare its judgments with human annotations on 200 trajectories, finding 96% task-success agreement. Human judgments support retry gains over Base and confirm adoption after checking on audited tasks. We release trajectories, versioned scoring, and reference and delivery audits. These findings highlight evidence availability and answer selection as complementary dimensions of agent reliability.

</details>


### [126] [From Learner Behavior to Reusable Skills for Effective and Efficient Learner Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37157)
*Zijian Chen,Zheng Zhang,Miao Jia,Xingchen Hu,Weibo Gao,Linan Yue*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Learner2Skill，将学习行为模拟能力外化为可复用的Simulation Skill，降低Token成本并实现跨LLM复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法需重复处理不断增长的历史交互以重建学习者，导致上下文和推理成本高，且模拟能力难以在不同大语言模型间复用。

Method: 提出Learner2Skill框架，将历史交互中获得的能力外化为持久、可复用的Simulation Skill；该Skill捕获学习者当前状态及响应模式，随新交互进化，并通过轻量级执行器校准适配新LLM，无需从头重建。

Result: 实验表明，Learner2Skill更忠实还原细粒度学习者行为，降低了总体Token消耗，且构建的Skills可在不同LLM执行器间有效复用。

Conclusion: 通过外化模拟能力为可复用Skill，解决了高成本和低复用性问题，提升了学习者行为模拟的效率与泛化性。

Abstract: Learner simulation aims to reproduce how a particular learner behaves on new tasks. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate increasingly fine-grained learning behaviors, existing approaches often need to repeatedly process a growing interaction history to reconstruct the learner. This introduces additional context and inference costs and makes the acquired learner-specific simulation capability difficult to reuse across different LLMs. We therefore propose Learner2Skill, which externalizes the simulation capability acquired from historical interactions into a persistent and reusable Simulation Skill. The Skill captures the learner's current learning state and recurring response patterns, evolves as new real interactions arrive, and can be adapted to a new LLM through lightweight executor calibration without reconstructing the learner from scratch. Experiments show that Learner2Skill more faithfully reproduces fine-grained learner behavior while reducing overall token cost, and that the same constructed Skills can be effectively reused across different LLM executors.

</details>


### [127] [SimpleEvol: An Agent-Loop Framework for LLM-Driven Automated Heuristic Design with Minimal Human Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37172)
*Jianghan Zhu,Cong Zhang,Rongjie Zhu,Chi Zhang,Zhiguang Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文批评了当前基于大型语言模型（LLMs）的自动化启发式设计（AHD）中过度依赖人工经验（human priors）的范式，提出了衡量框架“手工化程度”和“智力转化效率”的新指标。通过实验发现，减少人工先验能显著提升LLM的效果，并据此提出了完全自主的SimpleEvol框架，证明了轻量化、模型中心的方法优于复杂的手工流水线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的AHD将LLM视为固定组件（如交叉或变异算子），嵌入在重度人工设计的进化框架中。这种做法误解了LLM作为通用推理者的能力，限制了其在低级操作中的作用，且违反了“痛苦教训”原则（即随计算量扩展的一般方法优于手工定制方案）。旨在探索如何更好地将强大的LLM能力转化为高效的启发式算法。

Method: 1. 提出两个新指标：框架手工化程度（AHI）和智力转化效率（ICE）。
2. 评估十种LLM在三个组合优化问题上的表现，分析人工先验与性能的关系。
3. 提出SimpleEvol：一种基于智能体循环（agent-loop）的AHD框架，移除几乎所有人工先验，使LLM自主运作。
4. 对比验证SimpleEvol与其他复杂AHD管道的效果。

Result: 1. 实验表明，具有较少人工先验的框架 consistently 产生更高的智力转化效率（ICE）。
2. SimpleEvol框架在所有测试场景下均实现了最高的ICE，且优势显著。
3. 结果挑战了构建复杂AHD管道的趋势，证明了减少人工干预是放大模型智能更有效的手段。

Conclusion: 应转向更轻量化、以模型为中心的AHD方法。通过最小化人工先验并赋予LLM自主权，可以更有效利用模型能力提升启发式设计水平，而非依赖复杂的人工工程管道。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have emerged as powerful tools for automated heuristic design (AHD), enabling iterative generation and refinement of heuristics. However, the dominant paradigm embeds LLMs as narrow, fixed components, such as crossover or mutation, within heavily hand-engineered evolutionary frameworks. We argue this misapprehends LLMs. It treats them as specialized tools rather than general reasoners, constrains them to low-level operations, and underutilizes their autonomy. Moreover, the extensive human priors in these frameworks violate the bitter lesson principle that general methods scaling with computation surpass hand-crafted solutions. This raises a key question: which AHD framework designs best convert stronger LLM capabilities into better heuristics? To address this, we propose metrics for LLM-driven AHD framework handcraftedness (AHI) and intelligence conversion efficiency (ICE). Evaluating ten LLMs across three challenging combinatorial optimization problems, we obtain a notable finding that frameworks with fewer human priors consistently yield higher ICE. Based on this finding, we propose SimpleEvol, an agent-loop framework for AHD which removes nearly all human priors and allows the LLM to operate autonomously. SimpleEvol consistently achieves the highest ICE, often by a large margin. Our results challenge the trend toward complex AHD pipelines and point to a lighter and more model-centric alternative, suggesting that reducing human priors is a more effective strategy to scale up with model intelligence. The source code is available at https://github.com/HenryZhu1029/SimpleEvol-Master.

</details>


### [128] [Absorbed in Inertia: Activation Analysis for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37176)
*Giulio Segalini,Zhi Wen Soi,Jérémie Decouchant,Lydia Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了计算机使用代理（Computer-use agents）存在的“惯性”问题，即代理在明知行动无效时仍重复无用操作。通过研究发现这是由于激活值陷入吸收区域所致，并提出了R³方法（重置、重定向、恢复）来解决此问题，显著降低了代理的惯性行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于自然语言指令在真实桌面上执行任务的计算机使用代理能力增强其潜在缺陷逐渐显现。作者发现这些代理存在一种隐蔽的“惯性”现象，即尽管意识到当前动作无效，却仍重复无果的操作，严重影响任务效率。旨在深入理解这一现象机制并提出有效缓解方案。

Method: 1. 提出协议测量代理内部状态（激活值）与外在行为惯性之间的关系；2. 对高维激活状态进行广泛分析，识别惯性对应的激活空间吸收区域；3. 提出R³（Reset, Reroute, Restore）框架：暂时重置上下文轨迹以跳出吸收区域，随后恢复历史上下文以完成任务。

Result: 实验表明，R³方法使不同模型中的测量惯性降低了17-55%。研究证实，通过改变上下文来中断循环比直接引导生成的激活值更为有效。

Conclusion: 计算机使用代理的惯性表现为激活空间的吸收区域特性。通过动态调整上下文策略（如R³方法）可以有效打破这种惯性，提升代理在多步骤任务中的执行效率和鲁棒性。这为解决基于屏幕轨迹推理的代理模型中普遍存在的重复低效行为提供了新视角。

Abstract: Computer-use agents have become increasingly capable of executing tasks on live desktops through natural-language instructions, based on trajectories of screenshots, actions, and reasoning. We discover that they can stealthily exhibit inertia, in which they repeat fruitless actions despite recognizing that these actions are ineffective. We hypothesize that inertia is reflected in the agent's internal state, i.e., the activation values of the agent's underlying model, and propose a protocol to measure the relationship between the two. Extensive analysis of high-dimensional activation states shows that inertia corresponds to an absorbing region of activation space, where activation values become stale across actions and even after attempts to steer them. We conjecture that drastically changing the agents' activations by re-initializing them is necessary to escape inertia. Specifically, we propose R$^3$ (Reset, Reroute, Restore), which temporarily resets the agent's context trajectory to escape the absorbing region and then restores the historical context to effectively complete the task. Our approach yields 17-55% lower measured inertia across models relative to unmodified agents. These results suggest that changing the context can interrupt recurrence more effectively than directly steering the resulting activations. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/vlm-agent-defense-D076

</details>


### [129] [Accelerated surrogate dynamics for dynamical, stochastic system evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37184)
*Marco Jochum,Ioannis Kouroudis,Gohar Ali Siddiqui,Taher Amine Hamzaoui,Manuel Gößwein,Alessio Gagliardi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合变分自编码器（VAE）与时间融合变换器（TFT）的框架，作为高维随机系统动态模拟的高效机器学习代理模型。该框架通过VAE降低维度，利用TFT捕捉长短时依赖关系及静态协变量，在保持模拟精度的同时显著降低计算成本，并提供不确定性量化以支持实验设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态模拟计算成本高昂，尤其在随机框架下；现有机器学习代理模型面临两大局限：一是系统高维性使传统时间序列模型难以处理高维特征空间；二是传统模型仅关注长期或短期效应，导致随时间推移产生局部或全局漂移。

Method: 构建混合深度学习框架：首先使用具有卷积或图基底的变分自编码器（VAE）对系统进行降维，生成潜在向量；随后采用时间融合变换器（Temporal Fusion Transformer, TFT）模型在时间维度上传播该潜在向量，该模型兼具编码长期和短期效应能力，并支持静态协变量输入。

Result: 在三个不同案例中进行测试，结果显示该框架生成的结果与原始模拟结果几乎一致，但计算时间大幅减少；证明了框架的鲁棒性、灵活性及可适配性。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效解决了高维系统和传统时间序列模型的局限性，不仅实现了高效且高精度的动态模拟替代，还内建了不确定性量化机制，有利于目标实验设计，具备良好的通用性和应用前景。

Abstract: Dynamic simulations are an entrenched way of gaining insight into the evolution of system dynamics. Their computational cost however is often prohibitively high, especially in cases of stochastic frameworks. Machine learning algorithms are especially suited as simulation surrogates. Nevertheless, they face some very distinct limitations. Firstly, the sheer dimensionality of these systems, however, precludes the use of traditional time series models who struggle with high dimensional feature spaces. Additionally, traditional time series focus exclusively on either long or short range effects, causing local or global drift given enough time. In this paper, we propose a framework that addresses those limitations. Our framework combines a Variational Autoencoder, with a convolutional or graph basis that reduces the dimensionality of the system. This latent vector is propagated in time using a Temporal Fusion Transformer model, which includes both long range and short range effect encoding, as well as static covariate support. We test our framework on three distinct cases, to prove its robustness and in all three we have achieved practically identical to the simulation results at a fraction of the time. Further, our framework is flexible enough to be adapted to any new system and provides an inbuilt uncertainty quantification for targeted experiment design.

</details>


### [130] [V-Engram: Trigger-Indexed External Memory for Modular Text-to-Image Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37198)
*Haoran He,Runyuan Cai,Yiming Wang,Lin Yu,Xiaodong Zeng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出V-Engram，一种基于触发索引的外部记忆机制，用于在冻结的Stable Diffusion 3.5模型中高效检索特定视觉身份，无需修改骨干网络权重，实现了高保真度与组合控制能力的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预训练文生图模型难以在少量参考下可靠获取特定视觉身份并保持组合控制。Token嵌入方法容易欠拟合，而适配器方法虽提升保真度但存储成本高且干扰概念组合。

Method: 引入V-Engram，为每个概念分配显式触发器以检索特定记忆；通过门控方向将记忆作为相对残差注入冻结的文本编码器和MMDiT上下文状态；分离记忆与骨干适配，支持提示符选择和多概念访问。

Result: V-Engram在整体主体保真度上与DreamBooth-LoRA相当，但在上下文主体保留等场景中具优势；单概念查询时仅加载匹配的条目，显著减少额外状态加载；支持配对触发组合及同类分离，未注册提示保持原模型行为。

Conclusion: 触发索引记忆作为模块化接口，可在不重写生成器的情况下添加针对性视觉证据，解决了身份获取与组合控制的权衡问题。

Abstract: Pretrained text-to-image models contain broad visual knowledge, yet they cannot reliably acquire or refine a specific visual identity from only a few references while preserving compositional control. Token-embedding methods are compact but often underfit identity, whereas adapter-based methods improve fidelity through persistent weight updates that can be costly to store and interfere when concepts are composed. We introduce V-Engram, a trigger-indexed external memory mechanism for Stable Diffusion 3.5. Each concept is assigned an explicit trigger that retrieves concept-specific memory, whose gated directions enter frozen text-encoder and MMDiT context states as relative residuals. Separating this memory from backbone adaptation enables prompt-selective and multi-concept access without merging model updates. Experiments show that V-Engram broadly matches DreamBooth-LoRA in overall subject fidelity while showing advantages in settings such as contextual subject preservation. Prompt-matched loading retrieves only matched entries, reducing most additional adaptation-state loading for a single-concept query. Qualitative results further demonstrate paired-trigger composition and same-class separation, while prompts without registered entries retain the frozen model's base behavior. Together, these results establish trigger-indexed memory as a modular interface for adding targeted visual evidence without rewriting the generator.

</details>


### [131] [Learning to Prove, Not Just to Answer: Reinforcement Learning from Formal Verification for Natural-Language Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37203)
*Qili Zhang,Qianren Mao,Hanze Cai,Kaiming Zhao,Yuening He,Xihan Lei,Yashuo Luo,Hanwen Hao,Yutong Gu,Likang Xiao,Zhijun Chen,Weifeng Jiang,Haoyi Zhou,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Proof-R1框架，通过形式化验证和RL训练LLM，实现自然语言逻辑推理中中间结论的机器可检查验证及答案依赖关系的对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对中间结论和证明依赖关系的机器可检查验证，容易错误地给予无效或无关步骤的认可。

Method: Proof-R1采用基于强化学习的框架，利用UNSAT基础的形式化验证机制确保推理动作满足证明义务，并恢复支持答案的依赖闭包以追踪证明结构。

Result: 在三个逻辑推理基准测试和四种骨干模型上提升了答案准确率，并在推理过程可验证性方面优于免训练智能体和基于训练的方法。

Conclusion: Proof-R1能有效提升LLM在自然语言逻辑推理中的准确性和过程可验证性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed for natural-language logical reasoning, where the final answer is easy to check but the proof behind it is not. In natural-language logical reasoning, an intermediate conclusion should follow from its premises, and the resulting derivation should support the final answer. Existing methods lack machine-checkable verification of intermediate conclusions and answer-supporting proof dependencies, so they may assign credit to invalid or answer-irrelevant steps. We propose Proof-R1, an RL framework from formal verification that trains LLMs to construct verifiable proofs for natural-language logical reasoning. Proof-R1 admits a generated conclusion into the verified proof state only when the corresponding reasoning action satisfies the proof obligations through UNSAT-based machine-checkable formal verification. Proof-R1 also recovers the answer-supporting dependency closure to trace the proof structure of the final answer and align outcome credit with the proof dependencies. Experiments demonstrate that Proof-R1 improves answer accuracy across three logical reasoning benchmarks and four backbone models and outperforms training-free agents and training-based methods in terms of reasoning-process verifiability.

</details>


### [132] [Information Bottleneck-Guided Adaptive Hypergraph Transformer for Brain Disease Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37220)
*Jingxi Feng,Xudong Chen,Yifan Zhang,Heming Xu,Hongcheng Han,Xijing Wang,Dong Zhang,Shaoyi Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IBAHGT模型，融合信息瓶颈原理、自适应超图卷积与Transformer编码器，解决脑网络中高阶相关性与长距离依赖信息的冗余及未统一整合问题，实现高精度脑疾病诊断及生物标志物识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏高阶与长距离依赖信息的统一整合，且存在大量冗余，限制了其在脑疾病诊断中的有效性。

Method: 提出IBAHGT框架：1) 基于信息瓶颈的自适应超图卷积（HIB），学习节点与超边间的传递权重，捕捉最大信息最小冗余(MIMR)的高阶信息；2) Transformer编码器利用注意力机制建模短/长距离全局依赖；3) 信息瓶颈引导的节点级自适应融合，独立加权融合高阶与全局信息。

Result: 实验表明该方法优于当前最先进方法，能有效识别用于临床应用的生物标志物。

Conclusion: IBAHGT通过统一框架高效整合高阶与长程依赖信息，为脑网络分析和脑疾病诊断提供了更精确有效的新方案。

Abstract: Exploring high-order correlations and long-range dependencies in brain networks holds significant value for both neuroscience research and clinical diagnosis. However, previous studies have lacked a unified integration of high-order and long-range dependency information in brain networks, and there is substantial redundancy behind various types of information. These issues limit their effectiveness in the diagnosis of brain diseases. To address this, we propose an Information Bottleneck-Guided Adaptive HyperGraph Transformer (IBAHGT). By incorporating the information bottleneck (IB) principle, this approach enables adaptive learning of high-order correlations and both short- and long-range dependencies within a unified framework for brain network analysis, achieving high-precision brain disease diagnosis. IBAHGT consists of three key components: an information bottleneck-guided adaptive hypergraph convolution, which introduces a novel hypergraph information bottleneck (HIB) principle to adaptively learn hypergraph message-passing weights between nodes and hyperedges, optimizes information flow and captures high-order information in brain networks that is maximally informative and minimally redundant (MIMR). The Transformer encoder captures global information within brain networks through the attention mechanism, specifically modeling short- and long-range dependencies. An information bottleneck-guided node-level adaptive fusion employs the IB principle to learn independent weights for each node, facilitating the fine-grained integration of high-order information and global information to obtain an efficient representation for downstream tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms current state-of-the-art methods and can identify biomarkers for clinical applications.

</details>


### [133] [OptiCom : A Unified Framework for State-Conditioned Composition in LLM-Driven Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37221)
*Chenxing Wei,Sichen Liu,Lizhao Liu,Ningyuan Sun,Chen Bingzhou,Ying He,Bo Jiang,Fei Yu,Yao Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OptiCom框架，通过分解决策机会与损失的理论基础，协调快速优化控制器和慢速策略适配器，动态组合LLM搜索机制，在32组基准测试中表现卓越，证明了其作为通用LLM测试时扩展范式的适用性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型语言模型（LLMs）被广泛用于迭代优化以解决复杂问题，但动态协调不同搜索机制（受候选质量、故障模式和资源预算影响）仍是一个关键挑战。实证诊断表明，机制的有效性高度依赖于状态。

Method: 1. 理论基础：将预期终端改进分解为“累积决策机会减去累积选择损失”。2. 框架设计：提出OptiCom统一框架，定义共享配置空间C=(A,Q,O,E,M,S)（工件、查询、算子、评估、记忆、策略）。3. 执行机制：由基于LLM的快速优化控制器通过结构化Action Packages动态组合即时机制；由较慢的策略适配器根据累积轨迹反馈优化长期选择偏好、算子权重和模板。

Result: 在32个基准组中进行了全面评估。OptiCom在14个评估配置中平均Max-score排名为1.72，并在23个组别中获得最高分。

Conclusion: OptiCom展示了广泛的适用性和高可扩展性，作为一个稳健的LLM测试时扩展（test-time scaling）的通用范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to solve complex scientific and practical problems via iterative optimization. However, dynamically coordinating diverse search mechanisms as candidate quality, failure modes, and resource budgets evolve remains a critical open challenge. Targeted empirical diagnostics reveal that mechanism effectiveness is highly state-dependent. Motivated by this, we analyze how individual decisions drive final outcomes, decomposing the expected terminal improvement under a shared budget into cumulative decision opportunities minus cumulative selection losses. Guided by this opportunity-loss theoretical foundation, we propose OptiCom, a unified framework that represents LLM-driven optimizers within a shared configuration space: C=(A,Q,O,E,M,S), corresponding to artifact, query, operator, evaluation, memory, and strategy. Operating within this space, a fast LLM-based Optimization Controller dynamically composes immediate mechanisms through structured Action Packages, while a slower Strategy Adapter refines long-term selection preferences, operator weights, and templates based on accumulated trajectory feedback. Comprehensive evaluations across 32 benchmark groups demonstrate the superiority of framework: OptiCom achieves an average Max-score rank of 1.72 among 14 evaluated configurations, securing the top score in 23 groups. Ultimately, these results highlight the broad applicability and high extensibility of OptiCom as a general-purpose paradigm for robust LLM test-time scaling.

</details>


### [134] [Asking for What Was Never Requested: Horizontal and Vertical Proactivity in Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37236)
*Ido Levy,Asaf Yehudai,Segev Shlomov,Asaf Adi,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Q&D方法通过无奖励模型训练提问者优先选择能获取更多关键证据的问题，实现了“水平”和“垂直”两种主动性的内容优化。在多个多跳问答基准上，该模型在同等检索成本下优于原模型及15倍大小的提示模型，并在客服模拟场景中展示了更高的任务完成率和更少的交互轮次，证明了主动性不仅在于“是否行动”，更在于“追求什么”及“何时停止”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理研究多关注代理是否应自主行动（时机），而忽略了代理应追求何种信息（内容）。用户明确请求之外的信息对于完成任务至关重要，但现有的主动性研究未区分不同维度的主动性行为。

Method: 1. 定义两种主动性维度：横向主动性（基于当前语境识别未陈述信息）和纵向主动性（基于早期证据揭示的新需求）。
2. 构建依赖关系图（Need Graph），从基准测试分解中恢复需求间的依赖关系，以便在不依赖模型裁判的情况下评分。
3. 提出Q&D（Questioner and Drafter）训练机制，训练提问者优先选择那些后续能检索到更多所需证据的问题，无需奖励模型或裁判。

Result: 在三个多跳问答基准的保留集上，训练后的提问者在同等检索消耗下提升了两种形式的主动性表现，性能优于相同角色但经过提示的同规模模型，且在两个基准上超越了一个大15倍的模型。结果在控制问题数量和长度后依然显著。在交互式客服和零售模拟场景中，未经额外训练的提问者完成了更多任务且提问更少，在零售场景中以更少的客户跟进轮次超过了15倍大的模型。

Conclusion: 代理的主动性不仅取决于它是否在被要求之外采取行动，还取决于它选择追求的信息内容以及何时停止追问。这种对主动性内容的精细化建模和训练能显著提升复杂任务中的效率和效果。

Abstract: An agent that uses tools typically responds to what the user explicitly asks, yet completing the task may require information the user never requested. Work on proactive agents mainly studies whether and when an agent should act on its own, not what information it should pursue. We study a distinct axis of proactivity: its content. Horizontal proactivity pursues unstated information that the current context already identifies, and vertical proactivity pursues needs that only earlier evidence reveals. A need graph, recovered from a benchmark's own decomposition, records which needs depend on which, so both forms, and whether the agent stops at the right time, can be scored from a transcript without a model judge. To learn this behavior, we propose Q&D (questioner and drafter), which trains a questioner to prefer the question whose continuation retrieves more of the required evidence, with no reward model or judge. On held-out splits of three multi-hop question-answering benchmarks, at equal retrieval spend, the trained questioner improves both forms of proactivity over the same model, prompted, and outperforms a prompted model $15\times$ larger in the same role on two of the three, and the gain persists after controlling for question volume and length. Without further training, we place the questioner in an interactive customer-service agent with a simulated customer, where it completes more tasks while asking fewer questions, and in retail it outperforms the $15\times$ larger model with fewer follow-up turns from the customer. These results show that proactivity depends not only on whether an agent acts without being asked, but also on what it chooses to pursue and when it stops.

</details>


### [135] [Foundations of Proactive Agents: Principles, Technical Layers, and Proactivity-Gym](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37267)
*Jio Oh,Seunghyun Do,Young-Jun Lee,Steven Euijong Whang,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了设计、实现和评估主动式LLM代理的框架（3T原则：任务能力、时间分配、信任），构建了包含五个维度的设计空间，并发布了PROACTIVITY-GYM仿真测试平台。实验揭示LJM在评估中混淆任务与信任，且用户更看重介入时机以维持专注而非单纯的正确性，强调需联合优化这三方面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动式LLM代理虽能利用空闲算力提供支持，但常因误解用户上下文、增加审查成本或破坏信任而失效，亟需系统的理论框架与评估方法来解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出基于“3T”原则（Task Capability, Temporal Allocation, Trust）的设计理论基础；构建包含任务范围、预判视野、激活触发器、处理时机和介入深度五个维度的设计空间；开发PROACTIVITY-GYM仿真测试床进行多日场景、状态环境及个性化模拟用户的评估；通过23种模型配置评估及30人的人类受试者研究验证假设。

Result: 1. PROACTIVITY-GYM成功支撑了对主动辅助后果的评估；2. 在不同模型- harness 配置下发现显著的3T性能差距；3. LLM评估者常混淆任务能力与信任维度；4. 人类研究表明，即使结果正确，介入错位也会导致信任急剧下降，且用户倾向于接受睡眠期间的非完美帮助以保持当前专注。

Conclusion: 设计主动式LLM代理时，必须联合考量有效工作产出、计算资源分配以及动态变化的用户信任，单一维度的优化不足以建立可靠的人机协作关系。

Abstract: Proactive LLM agents can turn idle compute into useful support before users ask. Yet even correct work can misread user context, impose review costs, or undermine trust. This work proposes foundations for designing, realizing, and evaluating proactive LLM agents around three joint principles (3T): Task Capability, anticipating relevant needs and correctly performing useful work; Temporal Allocation, allocating compute according to resource availability and when results are needed; and Trust, sustaining users' confidence and appropriate reliance on the agent. We connect these objectives to a design space organized around five dimensions: task scope, anticipation horizon, activation trigger, processing timing, and intervention depth, and specify the situation and system modeling needed to support its choices, including user and environment representations, backbone LLMs, and agent harnesses. Lastly, we propose PROACTIVITY-GYM, a simulation-based evaluation testbed including multi-day scenarios, stateful environments, and persona-conditioned simulated users that can evaluate the consequences of proactive assistance across interactions. Evaluations across 23 model-harness configurations uncover substantial performance gaps across 3T and reveal that LLM judges often conflate task capability and trust. A human study with 30 participants demonstrates the importance of the joint 3T optimization: participants show sharp trust declines after intervention misalignment despite correct outcomes, and prefer sleep-time assistance, even when imperfect, to preserve ongoing focus. Together, these findings support designing and evaluating proactive agents through the joint consideration of useful work, compute allocation, and evolving user trust.

</details>


### [136] [Task-Relevant Null-Space Residuals for Non-Injective Neural Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37272)
*Bizu Feng,Zhimu Yang,Shuming Wang,Yuan Cheng,Shaode Yu,Xiaojun Qian,Zixin Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出任务相关零空间残差（NSR）框架，解决非单射线性映射中因输入合并而丢失的任务关键信息，在令牌合并和图聚合任务上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非单射映射导致的“操作员诱导的不可区分性”与“下游任务所需的区别”之间的不匹配问题，即消除的输入差异可能仍对下游任务重要。

Method: 提出NSR框架，结合从预映射表示中提取零空间分量、成员级编码与门控机制，以及特定应用集成，以在保留原始聚合规则的同时利用潜在的任务相关信息。

Result: 1. 令牌合并：36种配置中34种优于基线，最大增益达31.51 mIoU；2. 图聚合：Tree-NeighborsMatch任务深度d=2--6时达100%训练准确率，并在异配节点分类和分子图回归上取得增益。

Conclusion: 零空间残差是补充非单射线性映射的有效且实用的方法，使下游模型能从原算子输出中不可见的输入差异中学习。

Abstract: Non-injective mappings in neural networks map distinct inputs to the same representation, thereby implicitly inducing equivalence relations in the input space. However, the input differences eliminated by these mappings may still be required by downstream tasks, creating a mismatch between operator-induced indistinguishability and task-required distinctions. For non-injective linear operators realized in the current forward pass, their null spaces exactly characterize these invisible input variations. We propose Task-Relevant Null-Space Residuals (NSR), a general residual framework for non-injective linear mappings. NSR combines null-space component extraction from pre-mapping representations, member-level encoding and gating, and application-specific integration to exploit potentially task-relevant information under downstream supervision while preserving the original aggregation or merging rules. We evaluate NSR in two structurally different settings: token merging and graph aggregation. In token merging, NSR achieves higher semantic segmentation performance than the corresponding compressed baselines in 34 out of 36 evaluated configurations, with a maximum observed gain of 31.51 mIoU points under strong compression. In graph aggregation, NSR achieves 100% training accuracy on Tree-NeighborsMatch at depths d=2--6 across three backbones, alongside gains on heterophilic node classification and molecular graph regression. Together, these results support null-space residuals as a practical complement to non-injective linear mappings, enabling downstream models to learn from input distinctions invisible in the original operator's output.

</details>


### [137] [Transolver-$σ$: Joint Spectral-Physical Subspace Modeling for Neural PDE Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37279)
*Haonan Shangguan,Hang Zhou,Haixu Wu,Yuezhou Ma,Jianmin Wang,Mingsheng Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Transolver-$\sigma$，一种基于联合谱-物理子空间建模的神经网络PDE求解器，通过同时捕捉物理状态和全局谱结构，显著提升了时间依赖问题的自回归预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到仅基于物理状态建模的求解器虽具有一步高精度，但在自回归展开中误差累积严重；而纯谱方法在后期步骤更准确。旨在结合两者优势，解决时间依赖问题中一步精度与长期可靠性不匹配的问题。

Method: 构建包含自适应物理状态交互和谱变换的隐式子空间，通过重新组合响应实现信息交换。具体包括：1. 切片残差物理注意力（SRPA），保留显式切片路径并学习跨切片交互；2. 轴分解傅里叶算子捕获全局谱结构。

Result: 在五个标准PDE基准测试中，相比最强基线平均相对误差降低33.4%，且在单算子模型基础上持续改善自回归表现。在耦合多物理系统、真实流体及燃烧测量数据（RealPDEBench）上亦表现出显著优势。

Conclusion: Transolver-$\sigma$通过联合建模物理空间和谱空间，有效平衡了一步精度与长期稳定性，证明了其在标准基准及真实世界复杂系统中的有效性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Neural solvers offer efficient surrogates for numerical simulation of partial differential equations (PDEs). For time-dependent problems, strong one-step accuracy does not necessarily translate into reliable autoregressive rollout. We observe that a solver based only on physical-state modeling can achieve lower one-step error, whereas its spectral-only counterpart can become more accurate at later rollout steps. Motivated by this observation, we present Transolver-$σ$, a neural PDE solver based on joint spectral--physical subspace modeling. Within each block, adaptive physical-state interactions and spectral transformations are modeled in dedicated latent subspaces, whose responses are recomposed to enable information exchange between the two representations. Within the physical subspace, we introduce Slice-Residual Physics-Attention (SRPA), which preserves an explicit slice-space identity path while retaining learnable cross-slice interaction. In parallel, an axis-factorized Fourier operator captures global spectral structure. Across five well-established PDE benchmarks spanning steady-state prediction and time-dependent dynamics, Transolver-$σ$ achieves state-of-the-art with a benchmark-averaged relative error reduction of 33.4% over the strongest baseline for each metric, while consistently improving autoregressive rollout over single-operator counterparts. Transolver-$σ$ further delivers strong gains on coupled multiphysics systems and real-world fluid and combustion measurements from RealPDEBench, demonstrating its effectiveness beyond standard simulation benchmarks.

</details>


### [138] [AssayRouter: Historical Utility Priors for Frozen Molecular Predictor Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37285)
*Dong Xu,Zhangfan Yang,Jiantao Wu,Shipeng Zhang,Zexuan Zhu,Jiangqiang Li,Jun Zhang,Junkai Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AssayRouter，一种针对缺乏训练数据的分子测定法的预测方法。该方法利用已有完成的测定法作为伪目标，学习候选模型的“后拟合效用”（即加入局部预测器后对发现损失的减少量），从而在无需源身份信息的情况下，从冻结的接口家族中选择最优模型进行组合预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验室常面临拥有大量标签（16-64个）的新分子测定法，但缺乏其训练数据和参数的问题。核心难点在于如何从现有的预测器库中，筛选出适合构建小型本地模型的部分输出。

Method: 1. **伪目标机制**：将已完成的测定法视为伪-targets。
2. **效用评估**：通过计算候选者加入局部目标预测器后，“保留集”（held-out）发现损失的减少量来标注每个候选者的价值（post-fit utility）。
3. **共享回归器**：训练一个不依赖源身份信息的共享回归器，根据候选者在支持集上的表现来预测其效用。
4. **预测流程**：对于新测定法，先通过冻结的排名选择四个来源，再使用分离的标签拟合凸组合器以生成最终预测。
5. **训练与验证**仅在ChEMBL-MT数据集上完成，并在六个冻结接口家族的24个外部回归测定法上进行评估。

Result: 1. **性能提升**：AssayRouter-C比基线方法Support-CV@4降低了0.0409的严格四次调用负对数似然（NLL）。
