This modelling choice enables coherent generation under full, sparse, or partial observations while exposing compact social-state vectors that can serve as an interface for downstream embodied-agent systems. We evaluate BRAID under a unified SMPL-based representation on social forecasting, tracking and in-filling, and response generation, using metrics that assess not only reconstruction accuracy but also realism, diversity, temporal alignment, and interpersonal coordination. We further analyse the hierarchical latent space, showing that it captures separable group- and individual-level structure.

</details>


### [172] [Context Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37725)
*Rulin Shao,Shannon Zejiang Shen,Junjie Oscar Yin,Yuetai Li,Minheng Wang,Hamish Ivison,Radha Poovendran,Nathan Lambert,Teng Xiao,Mike Lewis,Wen-tau Yih,Luke Zettlemoyer,Pang Wei Koh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出上下文语言模型（CLMs），通过将其上下文视为可自由更新的文件，实现原生上下文管理。该方法在多项任务中超越现有最策略，并在效率与准确性上取得显著提升，同时支持基于自然语言指令的引导及在线强化学习优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的上下文管理策略通常由外部框架控制，缺乏模型原生的灵活性。作者旨在赋予模型原生管理能力，使其能自主决定保留哪些关键信息，并解决多智能体系统中共存上下文的复杂性问题。

Method: 1. 将上下文处理为文件，允许模型进行无限制的写入和更新操作；2. 利用现有的零样本模型构建CLM；3. 设计通过技能优化循环进化的自然语言指令来引导CLM；4. 引入针对CLM的在线强化学习方法；5. 协同设计Suffix Cache Reuse以提升推理效率。

Result: 1. BrowseComp-Plus：准确率提升11.4%，FLOPs减少21.5%；2. EdgeBench（12小时）：得分提高5%，FLOPs减少59%；3. 24小时多仓库agent-swarm任务：相同算力下改进幅度增大65%；4. 上下文管理任务：留出集准确率最高提升35.9点；5. Qwen3.5-9B在BrowseComp-Plus性能提升47.6%，FLOPs减少12%；6. 相比SGLang，服务器端计算成本降低35%且性能持平。

Conclusion: 将上下文管理从外部控制转移至模型内在行为，不仅实现了更高效的上下文维护（包括上下文内学习和参数化学习），还通过优化的指令和强化学习显著提升了模型性能与计算效率，为多智能体系统和长期记忆任务提供了新的范式。

Abstract: We introduce Context Language Models (CLMs), language models that natively manage their own context. We implement this by treating the context as a file and allowing the model to make unrestricted updates to this file. This allows the model to learn what is most important to maintain in context, and naturally extends to multi-agent systems where multiple agent contexts coexist as files. Building CLMs zero-shot with existing models outperforms SOTA context management strategies across a variety of tasks: 11.4% higher accuracy with 21.5% fewer FLOPs on BrowseComp-Plus, 5% higher scores with 59% fewer FLOPs on 12-hour EdgeBench, and 65% greater improvement with the same compute on a 24-hour multi-repository agent-swarm task. Moreover, by shifting context management from external harness control to intrinsic model behavior, CLMs naturally enable both in-context and parametric learning of context-management strategies. We show that CLMs can be steered with natural-language instructions evolved through a standard skill-optimization loop, improving held-out accuracy by up to 35.9 points on a context-management task while reducing compute. We also introduce an online reinforcement learning method for CLMs, improving Qwen3.5-9B performance on BrowseComp-Plus by 47.6% while using 12% fewer FLOPs. Finally, we co-design Suffix Cache Reuse for CLM serving, further reducing server-side compute by 35% relative to standard SGLang at matched performance.

</details>


### [173] [Spatiotemporal Hyperedges for EEG Seizure Detection and Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37730)
*Hyunju Kim,Sheo Yon Jhin,Noseong Park,Nabil Imam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了HyBrain模型，利用基于Mamba的回溯网络和时空超边块来高效捕捉EEG中的癫痫发作信号。相比传统的动态图神经网络，该方法通过少量软超边总结时空证据，解决了成对边缘构建带来的时空耦合丢失和高训练成本问题。在TUSZ和CHB-MIT数据集上取得了最优的AUROC性能，同时保持了高效的训练时间和内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癫痫检测与预测具有临床重要性，但面临癫痫发作罕见、时间局部性强以及跨多通道传播等挑战。现有的动态图神经网络虽然能建模这一过程，但其成对的时步边缘构造方法忽略了构成癫痫的关键时空耦合特性，且带来了巨大的训练成本。

Method: 提出HyBrain模型：1) 使用每个通道的Mamba主干网络生成每个（通道，秒）的一个token；2) 引入时空超边块，通过软隶属度将这些token池化为少量的共享组嵌入，并将信息广播回各通道。该编码器同时服务于窗口级检测、一秒点式检测和致痫前期预测三个下游任务。

Result: 在TUSZ和CHB-MIT数据集上，HyBrain在所有报告的场景中均优于十种基准模型，特别是在长片段致痫前预测方面差距最大。此外，其训练时间和峰值GPU内存占用与最高效的基准模型相当。定性分析显示，单个学习到的超边能清晰地捕捉实际癫痫片段中从致痫前期到发作期再到发作后期的轨迹。

Conclusion: HyBrain通过引入超边机制有效整合了EEG信号的时空特征，在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了癫痫检测与预测的性能，为解决此类高维度、稀疏信号的时间序列分析问题提供了新思路。

Abstract: Seizure detection and prediction from EEG are clinically important but challenging because seizures are rare, temporally localized, and propagate as coordinated events across multiple channels. Recent dynamic graph neural networks model this by running a temporal model over a sequence of per-time-step pairwise channel edges. However, this pairwise construction misses the spatiotemporal coupling that constitutes a seizure, at substantial training cost. We propose HyBrain, which summarizes spatiotemporal EEG evidence through a small set of soft hyperedges rather than pairwise edges. A per-channel Mamba backbone produces one token per (channel, second), and a spatiotemporal hyperedge block pools these tokens into E_h shared group embeddings through soft memberships and broadcasts them back. The same encoder serves three downstream tasks: window-based detection, one-second point-wise detection, and preictal seizure prediction. On TUSZ and CHB-MIT, HyBrain achieves the best AUROC on every reported setting against ten baselines, with the largest gap on long-clip preictal prediction. It also matches the most efficient baselines in training time and peak GPU memory. A qualitative analysis shows that even a single learned hyperedge cleanly captures the preictal -> ictal -> postictal trajectory on a real seizure clip.

</details>


### [174] [ContextRender: From Execution Dependencies to Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37743)
*Savini Kashmira,Jayanaka L. Dantanarayana,Lingjia Tang,Jason Mars*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了ContextRender，通过持久化执行依赖图管理LLM智能体的上下文，利用Tool-Flow Analysis追踪工具结果的复用情况（observed reuse），结合近期性和语义相关性在固定历史预算内筛选关键信息，以低于最大上下文窗口的长度实现接近全历史记录的性能，同时显著降低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在执行长周期任务时会积累大量工具结果，将完整历史传递给每一步调用成本高昂；而压缩历史可能遗漏后续步骤所需的关键信息。现有上下文管理方法往往忽视早期工具结果在后续执行中的使用情况，导致必要信息丢失。

Method: 1. 构建基于执行依赖关系的持久化图来管理上下文。2.开发Tool-Flow Analysis技术，跟踪后期操作对早期工具结果的复用情况，生成“观测到的复用”（observed reuse）信号。3.设计渲染器（Renderer），结合复用信号、近期性（recency）和语义相关性，在固定的历史预算内选择保留的工具结果，并将被省略的结果存储以备后续使用。

Result: 在AppWorld和8-objective QA数据集上，使用6K历史预算（远低于模型最大上下文窗口），ContextRender优于所有基线方法。其任务性能接近或超过传递完整历史的方法，同时将平均推理成本降低了10.2%-32.2%。消融实验证实，“观测到的复用”信号能有效提升任务性能和后期重用的结果保留率。

Conclusion: ContextRender通过精确追踪工具结果的复用依赖关系，解决了长周期任务中上下文管理的效率与准确性平衡问题，以较低的上下文容量实现了高任务表现和低推理成本。

Abstract: LLM agents performing long-horizon tasks accumulate tool results that later steps may need. Passing the full history to every invocation is costly even when it fits within the context window, while reducing it risks omitting needed information. Existing context management methods can overlook how earlier tool results are used in subsequent execution, leaving needed information out of context. We introduce ContextRender, which manages context through a persistent graph of execution dependencies. We develop Tool-Flow Analysis to track how later operations reuse information from earlier tool results, providing a signal called observed reuse. A renderer combines this signal with recency and semantic relevance to select results within a fixed history budget, retaining omitted results for later use. Across AppWorld and 8-objective QA with three execution models, ContextRender outperforms the evaluated context management baselines using a 6K history budget, well below the models' maximum context windows. Within this budget, it achieves task performance close to or above that of passing the full history while reducing mean inference cost by 10.2%-32.2% relative to Full history. Ablations show that observed reuse improves task performance and retention of results reused later.

</details>


### [175] [Cross-Entropy Guided Routing in Mixture-of-Experts Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37751)
*Yury Nahshan,Nati Daniel,Jacob Goldberger,Yoli Shavit*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种针对稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型的Token误差监督机制，通过将路由亲和度与Token级误差直接对齐，在保持稀疏执行预算不变的前提下显著提升了模型精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稀疏MoE模型的随机器通过语言模型目标学习亲和度得分，并辅以负载均衡正则化，但这种目标未能提供路由亲和度与Token级误差之间的直接对应关系，限制了模型性能。

Method: 引入了两种形式的Token误差监督：1) 每个专家预测误差得分，其加权聚合与下一个Token的交叉熵损失对齐，且单个得分在Top-K选择前衰减亲和度；2) 直接将路由器的亲和度与模型目标对齐，无需额外头部或推理时修改。两者均采用Itakura--Saito散度或指数负对数似然进行对齐。

Result: 在两个稀疏MoE主干网络和四个多项选择题问答基准上评估表明，该方法在参数匹配的随机基线上平均提高约2.3个百分点的准确率，在ARC-Challenge上增益达2.94个百分点。

Conclusion: 提出的Token误差监督机制有效改善了稀疏MoE的路由性能，在维持原有稀疏执行效率和聚合策略的同时显著提升了模型准确性。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) large language models scale model capacity by routing each token to a small subset of experts. Their routers are regularized with load balancing terms and learn affinity scores through the language-model objective. However, these objectives do not provide direct alignment between routing affinities and token-level error. We introduce token-error supervision for sparse routing in two forms. The first form predicts an error score per expert. The affinity-weighted aggregate of these scores is aligned to the next-token cross-entropy loss, while the individual scores attenuate affinity before top-$K$ selection. The second directly aligns the router's affinities to the model's objective without requiring an additional head or inference-time modification. Both formulations use the Itakura--Saito divergence or an exponential negative log-likelihood for aligning affinities and token errors. Across two sparse MoE backbones and four multiple-choice question-answering benchmarks, we evaluate both supervision mechanisms. On Granite, our method improves accuracy by approximately 2.3 percentage points on average over a parameter-matched routing baseline. With stronger supervision, the gain on ARC-Challenge reaches 2.94 points. Both mechanisms preserve the native sparse execution budget and aggregation policy. Our code is available in the supplementary materials.

</details>


### [176] [OmniVCBench: Benchmarking Evidence-Grounded Multimodal Reasoning Towards AI Virtual Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37773)
*Manyu Li,Xunkai Li,Yongfu Xiong,Yi Liu,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OmniVCBench基准和AIVC-Judge评估框架，旨在从“解释”层面评估AI虚拟细胞（AIVC），弥补现有基准仅关注“模拟层”的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AIVC基准主要在模拟层运作，缺乏对模型如何解读实验证据及制定生物学假设的能力评估。

Method: 构建包含6,077个问答对的图中心基准OmniVCBench；基于布鲁姆分类法设计三个科学推理任务；开发条件化多模态大模型作为裁判的评估框架AIVC-Judge；引入模型衍生的硬负样本挖掘策略以低成本生成选择题干扰项。

Result: 在异构模型池中，多项选择题准确率与AIVC-Judge评分呈正相关，证明了该框架能有效补充开放回答式评估，全面反映模型性能。

Conclusion: OmniVCBench和AIVC-Jague为评估AIVC的解释能力提供了来源可追溯、基于图的中心化标准，并通过量化结果验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence Virtual Cells (AIVCs) are envisioned as scientific agents that simulate cellular responses, explain underlying mechanisms, and support hypothesis-driven discovery. Existing AIVC benchmarks, however, operate primarily at the simulation layer, motivating complementary evaluation of how models interpret experimental evidence and formulate biological hypotheses. We introduce OmniVCBench, a figure-centric, source-traceable benchmark for the interpretation component of an AIVC. It contains 6,077 curated single- and multi-subfigure question--answer pairs derived from figures and experimental contexts in the scientific literature. Guided by Bloom's taxonomy, we instantiate interpretation-layer counterparts of the AIVC Predict--Explain--Discover agenda through three scientific reasoning tasks. We further introduce AIVC-Judge, a task-conditioned MLLM-as-a-judge framework with category-specific, reference-aware rubrics for evaluating open-ended responses. A complementary Model-Derived Hard-Negative Mining (MDHNM) strategy converts plausible errors observed during model inference into MCQ distractors for lower-cost evaluation. Within the evaluated heterogeneous model pool, MCQ accuracy correlates positively with AIVC-Judge scores, providing a complementary view of performance alongside open-response evaluation. Code and data demo are available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OmniVCBench.

</details>


### [177] [Adam under Generalized Smoothness with Second-Moment-Type Stochastic Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37787)
*Ruinan Jin,Difei Cheng,Ling Chen,Jun Luo,Hao Zhou,Youzhi Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明了Adam算法在广义平滑目标函数下，仅需随机梯度的二阶矩信息（无需次高斯或几乎必然有界等强尾假设）即可收敛，建立了高概率和期望意义下的收敛率保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Li等人（2023）提出的开放问题：Adam在广义平滑目标下，仅依赖随机梯度二阶矩信息时的收敛性分析；现有分析通常依赖过强的尾部假设。

Method: 基于Jin等人（2026）的Adam自归一化框架，将停止时间和去预处理策略扩展至$L_0$-$L_p$广义平滑条件和广义二阶矩ABC条件；结合轨迹控制与罕见事件的多项式增长估计。

Result: 1. 证明了在$p<2$范围内具有高概率收敛率保证，置信度依赖为$\delta^{-1/2}$且步长因子对$\delta$仅为对数依赖；2. 构造了硬实例证明该$\delta^{-1/2}$置信度依赖是紧致的；3. 在$p<1$情形下获得了期望意义上的收敛率保证。

Conclusion: Adam在广义平滑框架下的收敛性分析不再需要强烈的梯度尾部假设，二阶矩信息足以支撑其稳定性与收敛性，填补了理论缺口并提供了最优置信度依赖边界。

Abstract: Adam is widely observed to remain stable even when the objective deviates significantly from global smoothness. Under the generalized smoothness framework, however, existing analyses rely on strong tail assumptions on the stochastic gradients, such as almost-sure boundedness or sub-Gaussianity. Whether Adam converges on generalized smooth objectives under only second moment information on the stochastic gradients, without such concentration assumptions, was identified as an important open direction by Li et al. (2023). This paper gives an affirmative answer under fairly general conditions: such tail assumptions are not necessary. Building on the Adam self-normalization framework of Jin et al. (2026), developed for classical smoothness and bounded variance, we extend the stopping-time and de-preconditioning strategy to the $L_0$-$L_p$ generalized smoothness condition and a generalized second moment ABC condition. Even when the stochastic-gradient condition provides only second moment information that may grow along the trajectory, the stochastic trajectory of Adam remains in a locally well-behaved smoothness region, with stretched-exponential tail decay under bounded variance and global smoothness. Consequently, we establish high-probability convergence rate guarantees over the full range $p<2$, with confidence dependence of order $δ^{-1/2}$, while the stepsize prefactor depends on $δ$ only through a single logarithmic factor. We further construct a hard instance showing that, under only second-moment information, this $δ^{-1/2}$-type confidence dependence is sharp. Finally, in the regime $p<1$, we combine the trajectory control with polynomial-growth estimates on rare events to obtain convergence rate guarantees in expectation.

</details>


### [178] [A neural network that maintains and retrieves memories based on context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37791)
*Hayoung Song,JeongJun Park,Qihong Lu,Giacomo Vedovati,Monica D. Rosenberg,Zachariah M. Reagh,ShiNung Ching*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究开发了一种具有情境调制工作记忆和情景记忆的循环神经网络（RNN）模型，通过贝叶斯推断推断情境。研究发现，低秩方式调节递归连接性的情境调制能最好地匹配人类fMRI数据，且基于内容和情境相似性的检索机制使模型比无此机制的模型 retrieval 更快、更符合人类大脑表征。结论是提出了情境在自然环境中调节信息维持和长期记忆检索的计算机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知前额叶皮层发出的情境信号可调节工作记忆和情景记忆，但对此调节过程的算法理解仍然有限。研究旨在揭示情境如何计算性地调制信息维护和长期记忆检索。

Method: 1. 训练一个增强有情景记忆缓冲区的循环神经网络（RNN）。
2. 模型观看自然主义电影，并通过贝叶斯推断持续预测 upcoming scenes 以推断情境。
3. 情境以低秩方式调制 RNN 的递归连接性（工作记忆基础），并模拟为带有自注意力的键值系统，用于编码情境和检索情境一致的记忆。
4. 将模型的活动模式与人类在观看相同电影时的 fMRI 神经响应进行比对；比较不同模型的记忆检索速度。

Result: 1. 当情境以低秩方式调制 RNN 递归连接性时，模型活动模式与人类 fMRI 响应最佳匹配。
2. 情境同时调制情景记忆检索：模型不仅基于内容相似度，还基于情境相似度检索记忆。
3. 该增强模型更类似于人类大脑表征，且在情境调制下，记忆检索速度远快于没有情境调制的模型。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一种计算机制，解释了情境如何在自然主义环境中调节信息的维持（工作记忆）和长期记忆（情景记忆）检索。

Abstract: Every day, people continuously infer situational context and adjust the way they understand and remember the world. Context, signaled by the prefrontal cortex, is known to modulate working memory and episodic memory, but the algorithmic understanding of this modulation remains limited. Here, we train a recurrent neural network (RNN), augmented with an episodic memory buffer, to infer context using Bayesian inference as it continuously makes predictions of upcoming scenes while watching naturalistic movies. When the inferred context modulates the RNN's recurrent connectivity (the basis of working memory) in a low-rank manner, the model's activity patterns best match neural responses in human participants who watched the same movies during fMRI. Context also modulates episodic memory retrieval, such that the model retrieves memories based on not only content similarity but also context similarity. This is implemented as a key-value system with self-attention, designed to additionally encode context and retrieve context-congruent memories. The resulting model not only better resembles human brain representations but also learns to retrieve memories like humans much faster than a model without context modulation. Together, our findings suggest a computational mechanism by which context modulates information maintenance and long-term memory retrieval in naturalistic environments.

</details>


### [179] [DIET: Deletion-response Expert Trimming for Video Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37829)
*Jiachang Zhang,Teng Hu,Bohao Feng,Songhang Shen,Wenqiang Wang,Hongqian Deng,Ran Yi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DIET框架，通过记录单遍全专家校准的删除响应来训练-free地剪枝视频DiT中的MoE专家，显著减小模型体积并提升VBench得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单次剪枝标准依赖静态激活或路由统计，无法捕捉专家删除后的层间重新路由效应，且全专家存储成本高。

Method: DIET框架执行一次所有专家的校准传递以缓存输出和路由器状态；重放候选删除过程以生成“删除响应签名”；通过最小化总体多样性损失（ODL）保留专家方向覆盖性，并结合层内局部搜索与层间回归引导预算搜索进行专家分配。

Result: 在LingBot-Video 30B-A3B上，剪枝50%专家后检查点从57GB减至30GB，支持单卡部署；VBench总分从0.7941提升至0.8115；在各种保留预算下优于现有LLM剪枝基线。

Conclusion: 基于删除响应的训练无关剪枝能有效解决视频DiT中MoE架构的存储成本问题，同时保持甚至提升模型性能。

Abstract: Video diffusion transformers (DiTs) increasingly adopt mixture-of-experts (MoE) architectures to reduce active computation, but their full expert storage remains costly. Existing one-shot pruning criteria mainly rely on static activation or routing statistics and cannot capture layer-level re-routing after expert deletion. We introduce DIET, a training-free expert pruning framework based on deletion responses. A single all-expert calibration pass records expert outputs and router states for matched conditional and unconditional tokens. Candidate deletions are then replayed from cached tensors, requiring no additional model forward passes. The resulting deletion-response signatures characterize each expert by the changes induced when it is removed. DIET selects retained experts by minimizing Overall Diversity Loss (ODL), which preserves directional coverage in signature space, and combines intra-layer local search with an inter-layer regression-guided budget search to allocate experts across layers. On LingBot-Video 30B-A3B, pruning 50% of experts (6,144 to 3,072) reduces the checkpoint from 57 GB to 30 GB and enables single-card deployment on a 48 GB GPU without fine-tuning. Under a fixed 284-case VBench protocol, the VBench Total increases from 0.7941 to 0.8115. Across tested retention budgets, DIET consistently outperforms competitive pruning baselines adapted from large language models.

</details>


### [180] [Can a Cacheable Decision Model Follow Rules?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37832)
*Dushyant Rajput,Nirdesh Chauhan,Siddharth Kosaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了Certo（一种小参数非生成决策模型）在不同编码策略下的规则敏感度。发现独立编码虽低成本但严重损失规则敏感度，联合评分器表现最佳但无法缓存；通过目标性反事实监督可恢复合成任务性能，但在真实规则上泛化能力未获证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究在将候选动作独立编码以实现缓存降本（提高性价比）的过程中，模型对规则的敏感度保留程度如何，以及是否可通过训练重建这种敏感度。

Method: 构建了四种实验场景：(1) 对比缓存评分与联合评分器的性能差异及重排序干预效果；(2) 使用目标性反事实监督测试合成规则任务上的恢复能力；(3) 在真实规则上评估修正截断混淆后的性能，区分短题和难题集；(4) 使用跨域真实文本混合数据测试泛化效果。

Result: 独立缓存编码导致规则敏感度大幅下降（recall@1从1.00降至0.24），联合评分器保持完美性能；反事实监督显著提升了合成任务表现；在真实规则短题中联合评分器显著优于其他方法，长题方向性优势不明显；跨域混合数据不仅无益反而降低了准确率。

Conclusion: 虽然缓存编码器可在训练分布内具备规则敏感度，但向未见过的真实规则源的迁移能力尚未确立；联合评分器尽管牺牲了缓存效率，仍能维持性能优势。

Abstract: Certo is a small non-generative decision model (Qwen3-4B): it scores candidate actions from their text and returns a probability, instead of generating an answer. The accurate design reads the state, the rules, and each candidate together (a joint scorer), so cost grows with the menu. Independent encoding lets each candidate be encoded once and reused across states (about 5x cheaper at 77 candidates), but separates state from candidate. We ask how much rule-sensitivity survives that move, and whether it can be trained back.
  Four experiments on Certo: (1) the tested conversion to cacheable scoring loses rule-sensitivity (recall@1 1.00 -> 0.24) while the joint scorer holds 1.00, and a shortlist+rerank rescue fails; (2) targeted counterfactual supervision restores strong performance on held-out synthetic rule tasks (paraphrase, counterfactual, composition; reproducible across seeds), though we do not isolate whether predictions depend on the supplied rule; (3) on real rules the added benefit is not established -- after fixing a truncation confound, the joint scorer wins significantly on the short tier (0.861 vs 0.500) and directionally on the hard tier (0.655 vs 0.483, n=29); (4) a matched cross-domain real-prose mixture did not help and reduced contract accuracy (-9.3, -16.2 points). A cacheable encoder can be made rule-sensitive on its training distribution, but transfer to unseen-source real rules is not established; the joint scorer keeps an edge at the cost of caching.

</details>


### [181] [Mixture of Self-Improving Branches For Agent Harness Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37834)
*Haoyu Dong,Yuhang Zhou,Zihao Lin,Yifan Wu,Bo Peng,Mingyi Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Lizhu Zhang,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于分支搜索和路由器的自适应优化方法，解决现有递归自我改进（RSI）中固定开发集导致局部最优的问题，通过动态调整子集和策略生成互补的harnesses，并在多个基准测试中显著超越Meta-Harness。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的递归自我改进方法（如Meta-Harness）使用固定的开发集和建议策略，导致进化沿单一搜索轨迹进行，增加了陷入局部最优的风险。为了使改进过程更加自适应，需要打破这种单一路径依赖。

Method: 将搜索组织为多个分支，每个分支拥有动态演化的开发子集和建议策略。具体包括：保留仅由本分支领先harness解决的问题，移除所有分支共同解决的问题，并根据自身搜索历史修订建议策略。此外，提出一个路由器机制，在每次执行前根据新输入从各分支的头节点中选择最佳的harness进行处理。

Result: 在数学推理（Olympiad-level）上相对提升34.8%，在Terminal-Bench 2.0上提升11.6%，在SWE-bench Lite上提升3.8%。所有harness选择及路由器配置仅基于开发数据，无需测试集信息。

Conclusion: 通过演化分支目标和动态建议策略，可以生成具有互补优势的harnesses，并利用路由器在没有测试集反馈的情况下有效组合这些优势，从而显著提升递归自我改进的性能和适应性。

Abstract: Harness optimization provides a practical setting for recursive self-improvement (RSI), where agent-generated modifications inform subsequent changes through execution feedback. Recent work such as Meta-Harness implements this process through iterative code generation and evaluation, but retains a fixed development set and proposal policy. These constraints channel evolution along a single search trajectory, increasing the risk of converging to a local optimum. We make the improvement process itself adaptive by organizing search into branches with evolving development subsets and proposal policies. Each branch retains development cases solved by more of its leading harnesses than by those of other branches, drops cases solved by every leading harness across all branches, and revises its proposal policy using its own search history. To deploy the resulting complementary harnesses, we propose a router to select one development-selected branch head for each new input before execution. Across mathematical reasoning and agentic coding benchmarks, our system achieves relative improvements over Meta-Harness of 34.8% on Olympiad-level mathematical reasoning, 11.6% on Terminal-Bench 2.0, and 3.8% on SWE-bench Lite, with harness selection and router configuration based solely on development data. These results show that evolving branch objectives and proposal policies can yield complementary harnesses whose strengths a router combines without access to test outcomes.

</details>


### [182] [Active Budget Can Kill Sensitivity: Diagnosing and Repairing TopK Sparse Autoencoder Reliability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37857)
*Zhenting Huang,Junnan Liu,Qianren Mao,Zhixing Tan,Bo Jiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，在扩展稀疏自编码器（SAE）字典时，罕见特征的稳定性会下降。通过实验确定激活预算k而非字典宽度是根本原因，并提出配对秩稳定化方法，显著提升了罕见特征灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决SAE中特征的可靠性问题：即同一语义在不同表面形式表达下，特征是否保持稳定，从而确保其作为解释单元的有效性。

Method: 通过分析TopK SAE的特征灵敏度进行实验，执行受控的宽度和k因子实验，研究TopK选择的几何特性（如活动边界距离），并提出配对秩稳定化方法以纠正截止处的排序失败。

Result: 1. 扩展选择性降低罕见特征灵敏度，常见特征较稳定；2. 激活预算k是导致退化而非仅由字典宽度引起；3. 提出的方法使罕见特征灵敏度提升8.83个百分点，同时保持重构和活特征覆盖率接近基线。

Conclusion: Wide TopK SAEs不仅应评估重构和稀疏性，还应评估特征在语义变化下的可靠性和边界几何结构，以实现稳定的可解释性。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) are increasingly scaled to wider dictionaries to recover fine-grained structure from large language model activations. However, a feature is useful for interpretation only if it remains a stable unit of analysis when the same meaning is expressed in different surface forms. We study this reliability question for TopK SAEs via feature sensitivity. Experiments demonstrate that scaling selectively reduces the sensitivity of rare features, while common features remain comparatively stable. A controlled width\(\times k\) factorial experiment identifies the active budget k as the root cause: the degradation arises from the selection boundary rather than dictionary width alone. We attribute this failure to the geometry of TopK selection. The active margin, the distance to the cutoff, predicts feature loss without thresholds. Guided by this margin diagnosis, we introduce pairwise rank stabilization. Our method targets ordering failures at the cutoff and improves rare-feature sensitivity by \(8.83\) percentage points, while keeping reconstruction and alive-feature coverage near the baseline. Overall, our results suggest that wide TopK SAEs should be evaluated not only by reconstruction, sparsity, and feature count, but also by feature reliability under semantic variation and boundary geometry for stable interpretability.

</details>


### [183] [Co-PiLOT: Constrained Physics-Informed Latent Optimization for Target-Driven Inverse Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37875)
*Mahish K. Guru,Mayank Nagar,Ayush vyas,Jan Bohlen,Roland Aydin,Noomane Ben Khalifa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Co-PiLOT框架，结合ViT编码器和扩散/流解码器进行潜在空间优化，以及MERIDIAN主动优化器，用于镁合金微观结构的逆向设计，在有限模拟预算下显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决物理系统（如分子、器件）逆向设计中面临的高维非欧几里得空间搜索困难、可行性难以编码及评估成本高昂的问题。

Method: 1. 构建基于Vision Transformer的编码器和多种解码器（潜在扩散、扩散Transformer、整流流Transformer），学习最小瓶颈变量z；2. 使用自分割方向编解码器将重构图像转化为晶体塑性求解器的输入；3. 开发MERIDIAN主动优化器，集成深度核高斯过程不确定性、失效感知可行性预测、流形感知信任区域和目标导向采集策略。

Result: 在约8万张EBSD衍生的微观结构数据集上训练，ViT-FMDiT模型实现了高保真重构（FID 27.86, MS-SSIM 0.178）；在仅160次模拟的预算下，该方法组合将相对目标误差较七种基线降低了3%–22%。

Conclusion: Co-PiLOT框架通过生成式编码解码与物理信息黑盒优化的结合，有效解决了复杂微观结构逆向设计中的计算效率和可行性问题，展现了优越的性能。

Abstract: Inverse design of physical systems (molecules, devices, microstructures) often reduces to optimizing a high-dimensional structure against an expensive black-box simulator. Direct search is difficult because the space is non-Euclidean, feasibility is hard to encode, and each evaluation is expensive. We present Co-PiLOT, a latent optimization approach that maps candidates through a generative encoder-decoder, uses the decoder as a learned validity prior, and searches the latent space with physics-informed black-box optimization. The framework is applied on the inverse design of magnesium alloy microstructure/texture. We develop a vision transformer based-encoder; paired with latent diffusion, diffusion transformer and rectified-flow transformer-based decoders on $\sim80{,}000$ EBSD-derived microstructure dataset to learn a minimal bottleneck, $z$. The ViT-FMDiT model ($z$=$768$) reconstructs high-fidelity microstructure images (FID $27.86$, MS-SSIM $0.178$), which our self-segmenting orientation codec converts into input grids for crystal plasticity solver. Finally, we introduce MERIDIAN, an active latent optimizer driven by deep-kernel Gaussian-process uncertainty, failure-aware feasibility prediction, manifold-aware trust regions, and target-aware acquisition. Within a budget of $160$ simulations, the ViT-FMDiT and MERIDIAN combination yields the best target-driven objective score, reducing the relative target error by $3$--$22\%$ against seven baselines (DANTE, TuRBO, BAxUS, CMA-ES, DDOM, SEIKO, DDPO) on the same decoder.

</details>


### [184] [Guide, Then Let Go: Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling for Sparse-Reward Agentic RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37898)
*Youling Huang,Tiankuo Xu,Jiaji Liu,Tong Zheng,Shuo Zhou,Shaotong Qi,Junchi Yao,Shiyang Liu,Hao Xu,Pengcheng Xu,Bo Huang,Hongyi Fu,Lin Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为GATS（Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling）的方法，通过动态调整基于策略蒸馏（OPD）的指导权重来解决长视界强化学习中的冷启动问题。当教师模型表现显著优于学生时提供指导，随着差距缩小自动减少直至停止指导，从而在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld上取得了最优的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对长视界智能体强化学习中依赖稀疏结果奖励导致的严重冷启动问题：早期策略往往无法完成任务，导致缺乏有效的奖励信号以进行学习。

Method: 利用基于策略的蒸馏（OPD为学生自己的轨迹提供token级别的指导，并提出了GATS机制。该机制根据师生性能差距自适应调整OPD项的权重：当差距较大时提供强指导，随着学生接近或达到教师水平，逐渐减弱并最终撤除教师指导。

Result: 在ALFWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准测试中，使用三种Qwen2.5师生配置进行实验。GATS在所有配置下均实现了最高的平均成功率，相比仅使用奖励的GRPO方法，在匹配的学生轨迹预算下提升了4.37%-11.87%。

Conclusion: GATS成功解决了冷启动问题，并能有效利用比学生更小的任务训练过的教师模型进行知识迁移。代码已开源。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for long-horizon agents typically relies on sparse outcome-based rewards. This leads to a severe cold-start problem, as early-stage policies often fail to solve sampled tasks, leaving little useful reward signal for learning. To mitigate this problem, we use on-policy distillation (OPD) to provide token-level guidance on the student's own rollouts. We find that the benefit of this guidance depends on the performance gap between the teacher and the student. When the teacher substantially outperforms the student, distillation helps guide the student through the early training stage where outcome rewards provide little learning signal. As the gap narrows and eventually reverses, however, continued distillation becomes less beneficial and may hinder further improvement. Motivated by this observation, we propose Gap-Adaptive Teacher Scheduling (GATS), which augments the student's RL objective with an OPD term whose weight adapts to the teacher-student performance gap. Specifically, GATS gradually reduces teacher guidance as the student approaches the teacher's reference performance and withdraws it once that reference is reached. This enables GATS to leverage task-trained teachers smaller than the student, since teacher guidance is primarily needed during early training. Across ALFWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld with three Qwen2.5 teacher-student configurations, GATS achieves the highest average success rate among the compared methods in all three configurations, improving over reward-only GRPO by 4.37%-11.87% under matched student rollout budgets. Code is available at https://github.com/Ricardo-H/guide-then-let-go.

</details>


### [185] [You Cannot Pick a Provider From the Price List: Market-Aware Routing for Open-Weight LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37902)
*Liang He,Jingbo Wen,Yixiong Chen,Yue Yang,Qizhen Lan,Kangning Cui,Xilu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有LLM路由器仅基于模型静态成本选择，忽略了“由哪个提供商服务”这一关键决策维度。本文提出FACET，一种在线提供商路由器，通过认证特定（提供商×任务）的可行性特征，实现安全、低成本的路由选择，验证了市场感知路由需同时考虑模型与提供商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型语言模型（LLM）路由器主要基于每模型的静态成本进行模型选择，但开源推理市场的引入带来了第二维度的决策需求：客户端在选定模型后，还需选择具体的服务提供商。现有研究忽视了同一模型在不同提供商间在质量、延迟、可用性和价格上的显著差异，导致无法仅凭价格表推断最优提供商选择。

Method: 1. **大规模实证测量**：跨多模型、竞争性提供商、多种任务类型及三次测量波次，对实时端点进行测量分析。2. **问题建模**：将同模型下的提供商选择 formulated 为价格接受者市场感知路由问题。3. **基准策略**：提出简单测地图策略（measured-map policy），路由至既质量等价又健康的最便宜提供商。4. **创新算法**：引入FACET，一种在线提供商路由器，具备以下特性：(a) 认证每个(提供商×任务)对的可行性特征；(b) 在服务于未认证臂之前，若检测到漂移或不确定性，则安全回退至锚点；(c) 容忍不完美的任务分配和稀疏反馈。5. **缓解偏见**：针对系统性评估器偏差暴露的质量信号信任边界，采用真实探针或审计进行缓解。

Result: 1. **测量发现**：相同模型在不同提供商间质量和延迟差异巨大；高价提供商始终更快，但价格不可靠预测质量或可用性；提供商可行性具有任务选择性（如某部署在知识任务上正常，但在多步推理上严重退化）。2. **FACET性能**：Live provider运行证实，认证机制可将实际流量从昂贵的锚点转移至显著更便宜的已认证端点，实现匹配质量下的成本节约并避免降级端点。3. **鲁棒性**：FACET在放松的部署假设下，能有效处理稀疏反馈和不完美任务分配。

Conclusion: 市场感知的LLM路由不仅需要决定使用哪个模型，还必须决定由谁提供该模型的服务。通过测量和动态认证提供商能力，可以在保证服务质量的前提下显著降低推理成本。

Abstract: Existing LLM routers choose among models using static per-model costs. We show that open-weight inference markets introduce a second, largely ignored decision axis: after choosing a model, a client must still choose which provider serves it. Measuring live endpoints across [nummodels] open models, competing providers, multiple task types, and three measurement waves, we find that provider choice cannot be inferred from the price list. The same model can vary sharply in quality, latency, availability, and price across providers; higher-priced providers are consistently faster, but price does not reliably predict quality or availability; and provider feasibility is task-selective, with one deployment nearly normal on knowledge tasks but catastrophically degraded on multi-step reasoning. We formulate same-model provider selection as a price-taker market-aware routing problem. A simple measured-map policy routes to the cheapest provider that is both quality-equivalent and healthy, yielding matched-quality savings while avoiding degraded endpoints. Because the map drifts, we introduce FACET, an online provider router that certifies per-(provider x task) feasibility facets and fails safe to an anchor before serving uncertified arms. Across relaxed deployment assumptions, FACET tolerates imperfect task assignment and sparse feedback, while systematic evaluator bias exposes a quality-signal trust boundary that can be mitigated with ground-truth probes or audits. Live provider runs further confirm that certification can move real traffic from a premium anchor to a substantially cheaper certified endpoint. Our results suggest that market-aware LLM routing must measure not only which model to use, but also who serves it.

</details>


### [186] [Pixels to Keys: Exploring Spatial and Motion Cues in Gameplay Inverse Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37907)
*Abhishek Pillai,Ekta Prashnani,Joohwan Kim,Iuri Frosio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究在数据受限场景下深入分析了逆动力学模型（IDMs）从视频帧推断游戏输入的有效性。通过对比不同架构和预处理方法，并采用细粒度评估指标，揭示了传统聚合指标掩盖罕见动作失败的问题，指出相机运动、延迟效应和按键频率不平衡是关键挑战，建议未来引入3D场景结构和长短期状态建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大型IDMs已证明从大量 gameplay 视频中推断输入的可行性，但现有研究未阐明恢复个体动作的关键组件，且常使用能掩盖罕见动作失败的聚合准确率进行评估。本研究旨在解决数据受限场景下的IDM性能瓶颈及评估局限性。

Method: 在 Trackmania 和 Cyberpunk 2077 中进行实验，系统评估空间运动特征、模型架构和训练目标对 IDMs 的影响。采用 per-key 和平衡指标（如 $F_1^{macro}$）进行细粒度评估，并进行逐动作层面的失效分析。

Result: 实验表明模型架构和预处理中的运动流提取至关重要；非平衡指标会误导评估结果；相同架构在不同机制游戏中表现不均；相机运动、延迟效果和按键频率不平衡是导致性能不佳的主要原因。

Conclusion: 为提升 IDM 在稀疏或受限数据下的性能，未来的实现需显式建模3D场景结构、长期状态，并采用合适的损失函数以应对动作模糊性和数据不平衡问题。

Abstract: Video games offer scalable environments for studying perception and control in embodied agents.Abundant online gameplay videos could supply demonstrations, but they rarely include player inputs for training. Inverse Dynamics Models (IDMs) have thus been proposed to infer inputs from frames. Large (up to 1B parameters) IDMs trained on $\sim$1K-2K gameplay hours demonstrate feasibility and cross-environment generalization at this scale, but researchers do not clarify what the key components are to recover individual actions and often report only aggregate accuracy that can mask rare-action failures. We study the problem in a data-constrained scenario to evaluate how spatial motion features, model architectures, and training objectives affect an IDM's outcome and we analyse our models on per-key and balanced metrics such as $F_1^{macro}$. Our experiments on Trackmania highlight the importance of factors like the model architecture and motion flow extraction in preprocessing, while also showing the limits of evaluation through unbalanced metrics. The application of the same architecture and training recipe to Cyberpunk 2077 reveals uneven performance across game mechanics. Our per-action evaluation and failure analysis highlight ambiguities from camera motion, delayed effects and imbalanced key-press frequencies that call for explicit modeling of 3D scene structure, long-term state and the adoption of proper losses in future implementations.

</details>


### [187] [GRFBrain: Graph-Structured Rectified Flows for EEG Dynamic Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37934)
*Haohui Jia,Zheng Chen,Jathurshan Pradeepkumar,Xu Cao,Yasuko Matsubara,Yasushi Sakurai,Takashi Matsubara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于图结构的残差流（Graph-Structured Residual Flow）框架，用于从EEG预测时变功能连接性。该框架将条件均值预测与随机残差传输分离，利用历史数据和图高斯源来建模结构化变异，并明确了区分确定性预测优势与残差传输效果的评估条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在利用脑电图（EEG）预测时变功能连接性时，需要同时建模依赖历史的趋势和跨通道的结构化变异。尽管条件流匹配（Conditional Flow Matching）为分布预测提供了框架，但尚不清楚引入图结构信息的源分布是否比各向同性噪声或强确定性预测器具有实际的预测优势。

Method: 构建了图结构残差流框架，将条件均值预测与随机残差传输解耦：1) 使用仅依赖历史的预测器估计未来的连接图；2) 通过基于拉普拉斯算子的协方差，利用过去连接性编码依赖关系的图高斯源作为起点；3) 使用条件速度场将源样本传输至未来图的残差，其中传输时间与物理EEG时间被明确区分。

Result: 研究识别并确定了区分“有用的残差传输”与归因于确定性预测、学习表征及采样效应的改进所需的具体条件和控制变量，从而验证了图结构信息在分布预测中的实际贡献。

Conclusion: 该框架有效地分离了确定性与随机性成分，证明了在特定条件下，结合图结构与历史信息的残差传输机制能够为EEG功能连接性的分布预测提供优于传统各向同性噪声或纯确定性方法的见解与控制手段。

Abstract: Forecasting time-varying functional connectivity from electroencephalography (EEG) requires modeling both history-dependent trends and structured variability across channels. Conditional flow matching provides a framework for distributional forecasting, yet it remains unclear whether graph-informed source distributions offer practical advantages over isotropic noise and strong deterministic predictors. We introduce a graph-structured residual flow framework that separates conditional mean prediction from stochastic residual transport. A history-only predictor estimates the future connectivity graph, while a graph Gaussian source encodes dependencies derived from past connectivity through a Laplacian-based covariance. A conditional velocity field transports source samples to future graph residuals, with transport time explicitly distinguished from physical EEG time. Our study identifies the conditions and controls needed to distinguish useful residual transport from improvements attributable to deterministic prediction, learned representations, and sampling effects.

</details>


### [188] [Video-RSI: Recursive Self-Improvement of Video Understanding Agents via Harness Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37950)
*Bingjun Luo,Jialin Guo,Siqi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Video-RSI框架，让视频理解智能体通过语言模型递归修正其执行harness，利用主动视频调查和成本感知进化解决竞争失败解释，在提升准确率的同时降低处理帧数，实现更优的准确性-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有执行痕迹仅包含当前harness获取的证据，无法解决失败的竞争解释，限制了智能体自我改进的基础。

Method: 引入Video-RSI框架，使视频理解智能体使用自身的语言模型修订其harness；通过主动视频调查重访原始训练视频，以额外观察测试竞争失败解释；采用成本感知的harness进化，结合答案准确率和视觉成本确定保留哪些可复用修订。

Result: 在视频理解基准评估中，进化后的智能体提高了准确率并处理了更少的帧数，与现有视频理解智能体相比实现了具有竞争力的准确性-效率权衡。

Conclusion: 证明了视频理解智能体通过harness进化潜力来改进其自身证据获取和使用。

Abstract: Video understanding agents acquire evidence through an executable harness that controls what they observe and how they use those observations. However, execution traces contain only the evidence acquired by the current harness, leaving competing explanations for failure unresolved and limiting the basis for self-improvement. We introduce Video-RSI, a framework for recursive self-improvement in which a video understanding agent uses its own language model to revise its harness. Through active video investigation, the model revisits the original training videos to test competing failure explanations with additional observations, grounding proposed changes in evidence beyond the existing trace. Cost-aware harness evolution turns these diagnoses into reusable revisions and determines which revisions to retain by considering both answer accuracy and visual cost. Across our evaluation settings on video understanding benchmarks, the evolved agent improves accuracy while processing fewer frames and achieves competitive accuracy-efficiency trade-offs against existing video understanding agents. These results demonstrate the potential for video understanding agents to improve their own evidence acquisition and use through harness evolution. Code is available at https://github.com/bingjunluo/Video-RSI .

</details>


### [189] [Topological Coherence for Self-evolving Multi-agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37953)
*Sen Zhao,Ruiqi Kong,Zuyu Zhang,Lifeng Shen,Xinyu He,Xu Zhang,Qinghua Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出TOCOMAS系统，解决多智能体系统中任务拓扑与责任、协作、记忆边界一致性的问题，通过在线自进化优化结构，在多个基准测试中提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽能联合优化智能体和通信结构，但无法保证责任划分、交接点和记忆访问边界与任务依赖关系保持拓扑一致性（Topological Coherence）。

Method: 引入TOCOMAS（拓扑相干多智能体系统），将任务图锚定于工具接口，将兼容任务节点组织为可复用的责任域，并推导依赖驱动的协作及受边界调节的记忆可见性；通过在线自进化机制，对智能体、协作和记忆策略提出耦合变更，仅保留满足结构约束且提升奖励的候选方案。

Result: 在BBEH、WorkBench、SWE-Bench-Verified和CoMemBench四个基准上，相比基线模型显著提升了任务成功率；在CoMemBench上，还验证了在已验证进度、交接次数和记忆隔离方面的优势。

Conclusion: TOCOMAS通过确保任务拓扑与多智能体结构的相干性，有效解决了复杂任务中的责任、协作与记忆管理难题，实现了更优的性能表现。

Abstract: Complex tasks inherently couple workflow structure, agent responsibility, collaboration, and memory access: task regions delimit responsibility and tool scope, cross-region dependencies give rise to handoffs, and ownership boundaries delimit private and selectively shared memory. Existing methods can jointly optimize agent and communication structures, yet such optimization does not by itself require responsibility, handoff, and memory boundaries to remain consistent with task dependencies. We term this requirement topological coherence. We introduce TOCOMAS, a Topology-Coherent Multi-Agent System. TOCOMAS grounds a task graph in tool interfaces, organizes compatible task nodes into reusable responsibility domains, and derives dependency-induced and profile-conditioned collaboration together with boundary-regulated memory visibility. During online self-evolution, TOCOMAS proposes coupled changes to agent, collaboration, and memory policies, retaining for subsequent tasks only candidates that satisfy structural constraints and improve evaluated reward. Across BBEH, WorkBench, SWE-Bench-Verified, and CoMemBench, TOCOMAS improves task success over baselines across backbones. CoMemBench also shows gains over the self-evolving baseline in verified progress, handoffs, and memory isolation.

</details>


### [190] [BrainNet Studio: A Unified Toolkit for Brain Network Construction, Intelligent Analysis, and Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37956)
*Xiwei Zeng,Shengrong Li,Yiheng Liu,Chunwei Tian,Daoqiang Zhang,Qi Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BrainNet Studio是一款用于静态和动态脑网络分析的集成工具包，整合了27种算法（包括深度学习、图神经网络等），提供从网络构建到可视化及LLM辅助解释的全流程工作流，旨在解决传统静态网络无法充分表征时间变化拓扑和高阶时空依赖的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态连接组工具在将动态网络建模与现代图学习及序列学习方法集成方面存在不足，且未能充分表征脑网络的时变拓扑结构和高阶时空依赖性。

Method: 开发集成化工具箱BrainNet Studio，包含统一的分析工作流（网络构建、特征提取、预测建模、生物标志物识别、可视化）；整合27种先进算法（深度学习、GNN、时空序列模型）；利用大语言模型生成个体和群体层面的功能/结构连接及结构-功能耦合的可验证摘要；支持在不重复组装特定管道的情况下配置任务、比较方法和扩展功能。

Result: 成功构建了一个实用且可扩展的平台，实现了高效、一致的计算框架，支持认知神经科学、脑部疾病探索性研究和脑机接口领域的连接组分析，并已在GitHub公开可用。

Conclusion: BrainNet Studio为脑网络分析提供了统一、灵活且强大的解决方案，有效弥补了现有工具在动态建模和现代机器学习方法集成方面的空白，促进了相关领域研究的深入发展。

Abstract: Brain networks characterize structural and functional relationships among brain regions and support research on cognition, brain disorders, and brain-computer interfaces. Their time-varying topology and higher-order spatiotemporal dependencies are not adequately represented by conventional static networks. Existing tools primarily focus on static connectomes and provide limited integration of dynamic network modeling with modern graph and sequence learning methods. We present BrainNet Studio, an integrated toolkit for static and dynamic brain network analysis. It provides a unified workflow encompassing network construction, feature extraction, predictive modeling, candidate biomarker identification, visualization, and assisted interpretation. The toolkit integrates 27 algorithms, including deep learning, graph neural networks, and spatiotemporal sequence models, to support classification and the identification of discriminative brain regions and connections. A large language model generates researcher-verifiable summaries of functional connectivity, structural connectivity, and structure-function coupling at individual and group levels. Within a consistent computational framework, users can configure analytical tasks, compare methods, inspect outputs, and extend functionality without repeatedly assembling application-specific pipelines. BrainNet Studio provides a practical and extensible platform for connectome analysis in cognitive neuroscience, exploratory studies of brain disorders, and brain-computer interfaces. The toolkit is publicly available at https://github.com/xbrainnet/Brainnet-Studio.

</details>


### [191] [SelfSearch: Reward-Free Search for Self-Improving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37968)
*Jungwoo Yang,In Jin Kong,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SelfSearch，一种无需外部奖励反馈的自搜索机制。LLM智能体通过记录并回顾自身的自我修改历史（推理、工具使用及结果）进行迭代优化，成功提升任务成功率并显著降低执行成本，在Terminal-Bench 2.1和SWE-bench Multilingual基准测试中表现优异，以极低成本达到或超越评估引导基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于重复下游评估来搜索改进智能体的方法存在两大缺陷：一是计算成本高昂，二是搜索过程被限制在特定的评估任务上。旨在解决这些问题，寻求更高效、通用且低成本的智能体自我进化路径。

Method: 引入SelfSearch，这是一种无奖励搜索程序。核心在于让智能体利用之前自我改进 episode 的记录（包括推理过程、工具操作和执行结果）作为具体经验，指导其后续的自我修改和优化，而不依赖下游任务的实时奖励信号。

Result: 1. **普遍提升**：在所有六组模型-基准设置中，SelfSearch 提升了种群平均成功率，单个智能体在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上最高提升 11.2 个百分点。
2. **效率与成本优化**：在 SWE-bench Multilingual 上，成功率提升 5.0 个百分点，同时执行成本降低 38.5%。
3. **低成本高性能**：仅需 \$4.03 搜索成本，即可产生一个在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上达到 82.0% 任务解决率的智能体集（Harness），性能匹敌顶级 Codex 系统，且优于评估引导基线。

Conclusion: 通过在自我修改过程中积累和利用经验，可以有效增强 LLM 智能体的下游任务能力和执行效率。这种无奖励搜索机制提供了一种兼具高效果和低成本的智能体进化新范式。

Abstract: Advances in the coding capabilities of LLM agents allow them to inspect and modify their own instructions, tools, and execution procedures. Existing approaches use this ability to search for improved agents through repeated downstream evaluation, which incurs substantial costs and ties the search to the evaluated tasks. We introduce \textbf{SelfSearch}, a reward-free search procedure in which agents modify themselves using records of previous self-improvement episodes. These records capture the reasoning, tool actions, and outcomes of earlier modification attempts, providing concrete experience for improving both task solving and self-modification. Without downstream reward signals during search, SelfSearch improves population-mean success over the initial agent in all six model--benchmark settings, with individual agents gaining up to 11.2 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1. On SWE-bench Multilingual, an agent improves success by \textbf{5.0} percentage points while reducing execution cost by \textbf{38.5}\% on tasks solved by both the initial and evolved agents. SelfSearch achieves competitive task success with evaluation-guided search baselines at lower search cost. With only \textbf{\$4.03} in search cost, it produces a harness that solves \textbf{82.0}\% of Terminal-Bench 2.1 tasks with DeepSeek V4 Flash under the settings of a public nine-harness comparison, matching the top-scoring harness, Codex. These results suggest that experience gained through self-modification can improve agents' downstream capabilities and efficiency.

</details>


### [192] [KV-Kaizen: Learning Context-Adaptive Cache Compression Choices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37988)
*Joao Monteiro,Louis Béthune,Anastasiia Filippova,Sonia Laguna,David Grangier,Marco Cuturi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出KV-Kaizen方法，通过自适应学习每层的KV缓存配置（深度、精度、秩）实现缓存压缩，无需丢弃token，在14B模型上减少32倍解码缓存大小且保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM处理文本上下文增大，KV缓存大小可能超过模型权重内存，导致吞吐量下降；现有通过丢弃不相关token的驱逐方法存在一次性决策风险，可能在后续产生不利影响。

Method: KV-Kaizen：训练一个选择器，根据上下文为每一层动态生成缓存配置组合，沿三个轴进行压缩：跨层共享缓存（深度）、降低缓存精度（precision）、截断低秩潜在表示（rank），并在预填充前运行一次。

Result: 在指令跟随和推理任务上达到准确率与缓存大小的帕累托前沿；相比基线方法，14B模型解码缓存缩小32倍仍保持准确性；7B及以上参数量下4倍缓存缩减无精度损失；压缩模型在同缓存尺寸下比小未压缩模型更准确。

Conclusion: 支持“先预训练大模型，后仅对其压缩”的策略，证明局部自适应组合压缩策略优于独立均匀干预或驱逐方法，能有效缓解长上下文下的内存瓶颈并维持性能。

Abstract: As the context size of text processed with an LLM grows, the size of KV caches can outstrip the memory allocated for the original model weights. This impacts LLM throughput negatively, since decoding is memory-bound and decode cost grows with cache size. Recent work alleviates this bottleneck by discarding the least relevant tokens. Eviction introduces a tension, since a one-off decision to discard content may prove detrimental later. Instead, we focus on alternative choices that can lead to cache compression without evicting tokens. We achieve this by learning a selector that is able to produce, based on context, a per-layer cache configuration towards an overall compression budget. The selector operates along three axes: sharing one cache across layers (depth), caching at fewer bits (precision), or truncating the low-rank latent cache representations (rank). We call the resulting method KV-Kaizen, for the many small per-layer choices it compounds. We observe that these interventions taken independently and uniformly over all layers limit achievable compression because they degrade accuracy. Crucially, composing them locally and adaptively to the context can instead preserve accuracy while achieving large memory savings. At inference, the selector runs once, before pre-fill. In evaluations on instruction following and reasoning tasks, our selectors reach the Pareto frontier of accuracy against cache size, against learning-free and post-hoc baselines. On long-context tasks, KV-Kaizen improves on eviction and can be composed with it, reaching a 32x smaller decode-time cache on a 14B model while preserving accuracy. A 4x cache size reduction incurs no accuracy degradation from 7B parameters up, and a compressed model is more accurate than a smaller uncompressed one with the same cache size. Together, these findings support pre-training large models and compressing them only afterwards.

</details>


### [193] [Which Attention Heads are like the Human Head? Not the Ones that Compute](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37991)
*Christopher Pinier,Gustaw Opiełka,Hannes Rosenbusch,Taylor Webb,Michael D. Nunez,Claire E. Stevenson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现大语言模型中与人脑EEG对齐的注意力头（attention heads）虽然在人眼注视等刺激处理上相似，但在因果必要性上远不如通过归因补丁（attribution patching）选出的头部重要。这表明“神经对齐”主要反映模型如何读取刺激，而非其解决任务的内在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在验证大语言模型中与大脑对齐的单元是否真正在计算过程中发挥因果作用，以及这种“神经对齐”是否能准确反映模型的内部任务求解逻辑。

Method: 在抽象模式补全任务（AAABAAA $\rightarrow$ B）中，对比LLM注意力头表示与人类EE数据；通过消融实验（ablation）测试不同头部子集移除对性能的影响；比较基于人脑对齐、概念向量（CVs）、功能向量（FVs及归因补丁）选出的头部及其对应的注意分布特征。

Result: 1. 对齐性与因果性解耦：与人脑对齐的头部的消融损害显著小于归因补丁选出头部的消融损害。
2. 头部类型发现：识别出强调独特元素的“新颖头”（关注人类注视点但移除后损害较小）和强调重复元素的“重复头”（对性能有 modest 贡献且与CV关联）。
3. 跨模型一致性：在3B-72B参数量级的17个模型中，FV-ranked消融均比brain-ranked消融更具破坏性。
4. 关联差异：脑对齐与FV得分关联微弱，与CV得分关联随模型变化。

Conclusion: 大脑对齐仅捕捉了模型如何处理和读取外部刺激（如人类注视），而未能深刻反映模型如何表征抽象模式或执行任务求解。高神经对齐并不等同于高因果重要性。

Abstract: Brain-AI alignment is often interpreted as a sign that model and brain perform similar computations. Whether the aligned units are causally involved in model computation is rarely checked. On an abstract pattern-completion task (AAABAAA $\rightarrow$ B), we compare LLM attention-head representations with human EEG and test how ablating those heads affects task performance. Alignment and causation dissociate: brain-aligned heads contribute to performance, but their removal is substantially less disruptive than removal of heads selected via attribution patching. We compare two head sets that prior interpretability work defines without reference to the brain: concept vectors (CVs), which represent abstract patterns across formats, and function vectors (FVs), selected for their contribution to correct-answer prediction. Brain alignment shows little association with FV scores, while its association with CV scores varies across models. Among brain-aligned heads, we find recurring attention profiles: one emphasizes distinctive elements (novelty heads), the other repeating elements (repetition heads). The novelty family tracks salience and attends to the same elements that humans look at, yet its removal is less damaging than random ablation on average. Repetition heads contribute modestly to performance and are associated with abstract-pattern representation (CVs). Across 17 models spanning 3B-72B parameters, FV-ranked removal is substantially more disruptive than brain-ranked removal. Brain alignment thus captures how the model reads the stimulus, and only faintly captures how it represents the pattern and solves the task.

</details>


### [194] [Diagnosing and Improving Probabilistic Reasoning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38005)
*Huaman Sun,Dingcheng Wang,Jason Hartline,Jessica Hullman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种决策理论框架，将大语言模型（LLM）的决策损失分解为信念形成和行为转化两个部分。通过合成基准测试评估了不同干预策略对这两个组件的影响，发现单独优化某一组件仅改善该组件而不一定产生迁移效应，而同时优化两者需依赖训练与评估格式匹配才能有效改善整体决策性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM被提议作为决策助手，需要在明确的决策成本下基于可用证据进行概率推理。然而，目前尚不清楚如何有效分解和优化LLM在从证据形成信念以及将信念转化为优化效用函数的行动之间的表现。

Method: 构建了一个基于合成基准（具有已知真实值）的决策理论框架，将LLM决策损失分解为“从提供证据形成准确信念”和“将信念转化为优化给定效用函数的行动”两个组件。在此基础上，应用该分解方法刻画前沿及开源模型的概率推理能力，并评估针对信念、决策或两者的强化学习（RL）干预措施，考察其对各组件的提升效果、跨组件及提示格式的迁移性，以及信念形成未改进时决策性能是否仍可能提升。

Result: 1. 针对某一概率推理组件的干预会重新分配决策损失，改善目标组件但不一定迁移到其他组件。2. 同时针对信念形成和决策制定进行联合干预可改善两者，但这一结果依赖于训练和评估格式之间的匹配。3. 决策性能的提升并不必然以信念形成的改进为前提。

Conclusion: LLM在概率推理中的信念形成与决策行为是可解耦的组件；优化策略需考虑组件间的独立性/耦合性及评估格式的一致性，单一组件优化无法自动带来整体决策能力的全面提升，联合优化则受限于训练与评估格式的对齐程度。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly proposed as decision assistants who must reason probabilistically from available evidence under explicit decision costs. We propose a decision-theoretic framework that decomposes LLMs' decision loss into two components: forming accurate beliefs from provided evidence and translating those beliefs into actions that optimize a provided utility function. Using a synthetic benchmark with known ground truth, we apply the decomposition to characterize probabilistic reasoning in frontier and open-sourced models. We further evaluate whether RL interventions targeting beliefs, decisions, or both improve these components across three domains, whether improvements transfer across components and elicitation formats, and whether decision performance can improve without improvement in belief formation. We find that targeting one component of probabilistic reasoning redistributes decision loss, improving the target without necessarily transferring to others, and that jointly targeting belief formation and decision-making improves both but hinges on matched formats between training and evaluation.

</details>


### [195] [HARISSA: Inference-Time Self-Checks for Efficient and Safe Local Language Model Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38006)
*Kenan Alkiek,Moontae Lee,David Jurgens,V. G. Vinod Vydiswaran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HARISSA是一种本地语言模型部署框架，通过利用预填充状态（prefill state）和答案状态（answer state）预测模型正确性，智能选择计算策略或转交人类，在保持隐私和本地部署优势的同时，实现了接近思维链（Chain-of-Thought）的准确率，但延迟显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决本地运行大语言模型面临的硬件限制问题：本地小模型能力较弱，而升级至云端模型会牺牲隐私和成本优势。需要在本地环境中解决两个核心决策难题：1. 效率问题——决定哪些查询值得投入额外计算以提高准确率；2. 安全问题——决定哪些回答是安全的，避免提供错误答案，必要时转交人类。

Method: 提出HARISSA方法，对模型进行微调，使其内部隐藏状态能够预测回答的正确性：1. 预填充状态在生成任何token前预测查询是否可被正确回答；2. 答案状态在生成结束时预测该回答是否正确。基于这两个预测，采用级联策略从低成本到高成本依次尝试回答方式，若预测失败则跳过，若最终预测回答错误则交由人类处理。

Result: 在单设备单模型测试中，HARISSA的准确率与思维链方法相差不到一个百分点，但延迟降低了2.7倍。在包含四种模型尺寸的服务器测试中，HARISSA在相同延迟下比FrugalGPT和Self-REF级联策略更准确；在相同拒答率下，其答案状态在六组任务设置中的五组比标准置信度信号产生的错误答案更少。

Conclusion: HARISSA证明可以通过利用模型自身的隐藏状态来实现高效的本地推理决策，在不依赖云端模型的情况下，平衡了准确性、延迟、成本和安全性，为资源受限环境下的语言模型部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Running a language model locally offers advantages in privacy, latency, and cost, but local hardware fits only small models, which are less capable than frontier models. The usual remedy for a hard query, escalating it to a cloud model, gives up the privacy and cost advantages of running locally. A deployment that stays local faces two decisions for hard queries instead. First, it can spend more computation on a query, e.g., reasoning before answering, which raises accuracy at a cost in latency, so it must decide which queries are worth the extra computation (efficiency). Second, some queries are beyond the local model, and delivering a wrong answer is worse than deferring the query to a human in the loop, so it must decide which answers are safe to deliver (safety). We show that both decisions can be made from the model's own hidden states. The prefill state, computed before any token is generated, predicts whether the model will answer correctly, and the answer state, at the end of the generated answer, predicts whether that answer is correct. HARISSA fine-tunes the model so that both states predict correctness, then makes both decisions with one policy that cascades through the ways of answering from cheapest to most expensive, skipping a way the prefill state predicts will fail and deferring the query when the answer it stops with is predicted wrong. On a device running a single model, HARISSA is within one accuracy point of chain-of-thought at 2.7 times lower latency. On a server holding four sizes of one model, HARISSA is more accurate than the FrugalGPT and Self-REF cascades at the same latency, and at the same deferral rate the answer state leaves fewer wrong answers than the standard confidence signals in five of six task and setting pairs.

</details>


### [196] [Brain-SAD: A Brain-Inspired Safe Autonomous Driving Control Framework with Dynamic Fear-Oriented Constraint on Dual-Policy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38016)
*Huan Rong,Chao Yin,Anouar Imel,Yijie Xia,Tinghuai Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Brain-SAD框架，通过动态恐惧导向约束解决自动驾驶中现有约束强化学习缺乏动力学特性的问题，实现更高效、可靠的碰撞防御与常规交互控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的约束强化学习方法（如Primal-Dual或硬约束方法）将约束静态化（如静态状态-成本映射或固定可行域边界），导致策略难以适应动态变化的驾驶场景，限制了其在不同交互情境下的泛化能力。

Method: 提出受大脑启发的安全自动驾驶控制框架Brain-SAD。该方法根据当前车辆交互场景生成动态“恐惧信号”，据此在线决策：在常规交互中生成长期策略，在紧急防撞中生成短期策略。恐惧反应被构建为动态恐惧约束，包括直接耦合行动影响的总恐惧成本，以及源自不同风险邻居的可行域动态边界，并用于在线策略优化。

Result: 实验表明，Brain-SAD优于现有方法，在任务完成时间更短的情况下实现了更高的成功率及更快的碰撞恢复时间，且在复杂度波动的连续交叉路口场景中表现出更强的可靠性。

Conclusion: 引入动态恐惧导向约束能够有效克服静态约束的局限，提升自动驾驶策略在复杂动态环境中的安全性、适应性和整体性能。

Abstract: Constrained Reinforcement Learning has recently gained increasing attention in the field of Safe Autonomous Driving, where the general mechanism is to maximize the expected reward while keeping the overall action risk bounded. In this way, the safety issues arising in AD can be mitigated through constrained actions. However, existing Constrained RL methods still lack dynamics on the imposed constraints. For instance, the action cost adopted by the existing Primal-Dual/soft-constrained methods is often defined as static state-to-cost mapping, and the safe-action projection in hard-constrained methods relies on the static projection with the fixed feasible region boundary estimated from offline demonstrations. The above drawback tightly couples the imposed constraints to the training scenarios, leaving the AD policy hard to handle different interaction scenarios, due to the improper state-level action-cost and the static projection boundary. Consequently, in this paper, we propose Brain-SAD, a brain-inspired safe autonomous driving control framework with dynamic fear-oriented constraints. By perceiving the current vehicle-interaction scene, Brain-SAD generates dynamic fear signal as fear reaction to online decide long-term policy for regular interaction or short-term policy for urgent-collision defense. In such two policy, the above fear-reaction will be constructed as the dynamic fear constraints, respectively reflecting the overall fear cost directly coupled with action-impact, and the dynamic fear boundary of the feasible region derived from different risky neighbors, both of which will in turn serve for the online policy optimization. Experimental results show that Brain-SAD outperforms existing methods, achieving higher success rate in shorter task-completion and collision-recovery time, and exhibits stronger reliability across continuous intersections of fluctuating complexity.

</details>


### [197] [PE-EK-PINN: Physics Embedding with Evolving Kernel for Scalable Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38023)
*Huiwen Zhang,Feng Ye,Chu Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有物理信息神经网络(PINNs)在处理大规模周期性结构时计算成本随规模指数增长的问题，提出了PE-EK-PINN方法。该方法通过“演化核”机制，将收敛的子系统场冻结并复用为高层配置的构建块，从而避免了重复推导控制方程，将训练复杂度从O(N)降至O(log N)，显著提升了大尺度电磁散射与天线阵列问题的求解效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINns仅通过损失残差嵌入控制方程难以处理具有高度振荡波行为的问题；而现有的架构物理学嵌入方法虽然引入解析振荡核，但其核字典需手动构建且随系统层级深度呈指数级增长，导致在处理天线阵列、超表面等高维层次结构时面临巨大的计算瓶颈。

Method: 提出PE-EK-PINN（Physics Embedded with Evolving Kernels），核心创新在于将物理核视为可复用的学习表示而非固定输入。具体步骤包括：1. 让模型自主学习基本单元场的演化；2. 当子系统场收敛后，将其冻结并提升为“演化核”；3. 利用这些演化核的变换副本直接构建高层配置，无需重新推导新的控制方程。

Result: 在偶极子阵列、复合线源几何结构和交叉阵列实验中，该方法不仅实现了更低或相当的相对L2误差，还大幅降低了训练成本。例如，求解256偶极子阵列的速度比直接PE-PINN快30倍以上，有效解决了大规模系统下的计算可扩展性问题。

Conclusion: 通过将物理核转化为可复用的演化表示，PE-EK-PINN成功克服了传统架构物理学嵌入方法在层次化系统中等规模扩张的计算障碍，证明了基于复用学习表示的结构化建模是解决复杂波动问题的高效途径。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) embed governing equations into deep learning, but enforce them only through loss residuals, leaving highly oscillatory wave behavior to be discovered by optimization. As a result, methods that achieve relative $L_2$ errors below $10^{-3}$ on standard manufactured Helmholtz benchmarks can fail on practical radiation problems involving singular excitations, absorbing boundaries, and wave fields spanning tens of wavelengths. Architectural physics embedding addresses this limitation by factorizing the field into analytically derived oscillatory kernels and learnable envelopes. However, the kernel dictionary must be manually constructed and scales with the number of elementary units, growing exponentially with the depth of hierarchically structured systems such as antenna arrays and metasurfaces. We propose PE-EK-PINN (Physics Embedded with Evolving Kernels), which treats physics kernels as reusable learned representations rather than fixed analytical inputs. A converged subsystem field is frozen and promoted to an evolved kernel, whose transformed copies are reused to represent higher-level configurations without deriving new governing equations. The resulting hierarchy makes the peak number of active kernels independent of system size and reduces cumulative training cost from $O(N)$ to $O(\log N)$. Experiments on dipole arrays, composite line-source geometries, and cross arrays demonstrate the dramatic training cost reduction, while achieving a reduced or comparable relative $L_2$ error. One notable example is PE-EK-PINN solves a $256$-dipole array more than 30 times faster than direct PE-PINN.

</details>


### [198] [Retrieval-Augmented Skill Optimization via Cross-Harness Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38024)
*Jaewon Chu,Ji Soo Lee,Jihwan Park,Dohwan Ko,Jeehye Na,Seunghun Lee,Taehoon Lee,Minseo Yoon,Minseok Joo,Yunyang Xiong,Hyunwoo J. Kim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RASO框架，利用外部技能库先验知识优化智能体技能，包含初始化（RASI）和更新（RASU）两个阶段，避免仅依赖昂贵的智能体执行rollout。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的技能优化方法忽视已积累的海量公开技能知识，仅依赖高成本的智能体执行（agent rollouts）进行迭代优化。

Method: 提出检索增强技能优化（RASO），通过交叉Harness适配（Cross-Harness Adaptation）从现有技能中检索相关知识并适应目标任务；包括无rollout的知识驱动初始化和基于执行反馈的迭代更新两个阶段。

Result: 在四个智能体基准测试和两个模型上的实验表明，RASO consistently 优于缺乏检索增强初始化和更新的基线方法。

Conclusion: 利用外部技能语料库作为先验知识可显著提升技能优化效率与效果，解决了现有方法忽视累积知识的缺陷。

Abstract: An agent skill is a reusable, actionable natural-language artifact that guides an agent to perform a task effectively under a given harness. Recent studies have explored the optimization of agent skills, contributing to a growing collection of publicly available skills spanning diverse tasks, domains, and harnesses. Despite millions of publicly shared skills, existing skill optimization methods largely overlook this accumulated knowledge, instead relying solely on expensive agent rollouts to iteratively refine skills for a target task. To address this, we propose \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Optimization (RASO)}, a framework that leverages an external skill corpus as prior knowledge throughout skill optimization. RASO retrieves relevant knowledge from existing skills and adapts it to the target task and harness via Cross-Harness Adaptation, accounting for mismatches in both domain and harness. RASO comprises two complementary stages: \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Initialization (RASI)} constructs a knowledge-grounded initial skill without requiring agent rollouts, while \textbf{Retrieval-Augmented Skill Update (RASU)} iteratively refines the skill by retrieving external knowledge guided by execution feedback. Across four agent benchmarks and two models, extensive experiments show that RASO consistently outperforms baselines without retrieval-augmented skill initialization and updating.

</details>


### [199] [UserProxyBench: Evaluating LLM User Simulators for Agent Benchmarks and Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38043)
*Ashish Jain,Armaan Sandhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UserProxyBench和用户保真度得分（UFS），用于评估模拟用户执行角色的准确性，而非仅评估代理表现，揭示了模拟用户在交互基准测试中的潜在偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式智能体基准测试和多层强化学习多将第二个语言模型扮演用户角色，但现有基准测试仅评分智能体，未直接衡量模拟用户是否正确执行其分配的角色，导致评估可能存在偏差。

Method: 引入UserProxyBench作为tau-bench系列的评估层，并定义用户保真度得分（UFS）。UFS基于任务接地的情结标准，独立于智能体成功率，专门衡量对基准测试中私有用户指令的遵循程度。通过固定智能体（GPT-5.5）并更换不同用户代理，在375个企业级任务上进行实验。

Result: 更换用户代理使平均任务奖励变化15.2分；24.4%的成功案例中存在用户规范违反。主要失败模式是“过早披露”（用户提前提供信息）。虽然这对任务奖励影响小，但在成功案例中导致智能体平均减少1.06次工具调用，从而改变了实际评估的交互过程。研究还确定了七个代理的经验成本-保真度前沿。

Conclusion: 模拟用户的保真度对评估结果有显著影响，特别是通过改变交互性质（如工具调用次数）而不改变最终奖励的方式。提出的UFS和成本-保真度前沿可帮助从业者在满足保真度要求的前提下选择最具成本效益的模拟器，以提供更可靠的基准测试结果。

Abstract: Interactive agent benchmarks and multi-turn reinforcement learning increasingly place a second language model in the role of the user. This simulated user controls what information the agent receives and when, yet current benchmarks score only the agent and do not directly measure whether the user correctly executed its assigned role. We introduce UserProxyBench, an evaluation layer over the tau-bench family, and the User Fidelity Score (UFS), which measures adherence to the benchmark's private user instructions using task-grounded rubric criteria scored independently of agent success. Holding the agent fixed at GPT-5.5 and varying only the user proxy across 375 enterprise tasks changes mean task reward by 15.2 points, while 24.4% of successful episodes contain a user-specification violation. The dominant failure is premature disclosure: users provide information before it is requested. This behavior has little effect on task reward, yet among successful episodes it causes the agent to make 1.06 fewer tool calls on average, changing the interaction being evaluated while preserving the reward. Finally, across seven proxies we identify an empirical cost-fidelity frontier, enabling practitioners to select the least expensive simulator that satisfies a required fidelity level.

</details>


### [200] [Probability is Not Enough: Exploring and Counting Divergent Tokens for Reasoning Uncertainty Quantification in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38070)
*Feiyang Li,Shengjing Liu,Qi Zhan,Sijie Cheng,Weiqing Wang,Hongwen Chen,Yuxuan Yang,Wen Wang,Yile Wang,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Divergent Token Confidence (DTC)框架，通过统计解码过程中两个模型在特定token上分歧程度（基于Jensen-Shannon散度）来量化大语言模型的推理不确定性，显著优于传统基于概率或文本校准的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型链式思考能力提升，准确评估其推理置信度以量化答案不确定性变得至关重要。现有方法多基于关键token概率，但其底层机制尚不明确，且本研究初步发现使用粗略替代值也能改善校准，表明需要探索更有效的置信度信号。

Method: 引入DTC框架，利用辅助模型（支持白盒和黑盒场景）与主模型在同一推理轨迹上评估下一个token分布，计算Jensen-Shannon散度以识别强烈分歧的tokens。仅统计这些分歧tokens的数量作为置信度指标，无需显式训练或影响生成过程。

Result: 在多模型家族和六个数学基准测试中，DTC提升了校准效果。白盒评估下，平均期望校准误差(ECE)降至13.0%，显著优于标准全序列置信度方法(32.7%-42.4%)。黑盒评估中，如DeepSeek-V3.2的ECE从32.1%-40.2%降至13.7%-16.3%，有效超越原始文本化分数。

Conclusion: 分歧tokens数量与答案准确率呈负相关，是量化不确定性的简单有效信号。DTC为提升大模型推理不确定性量化提供了新见解，代码已开源。

Abstract: As the chain-of-thought reasoning capabilities of large language models improve, evaluating and calibrating their reasoning confidence is becoming increasingly important for quantifying the uncertainty of their answers. Current methods for estimating the confidence of large language models are generally based on probabilities of selected key tokens, but the underlying mechanism remains unclear. Our pilot study finds that replacing selected token probabilities with coarse substitutes can also improve calibration, motivating us to further explore effective signals of model confidence. We introduce Divergent Token Confidence (DTC), a framework that estimates confidence by counting tokens at which two models strongly disagree during decoding. DTC identifies these divergent tokens using the Jensen-Shannon divergence between next-token distributions evaluated along the same reasoning trajectory. We find that their count is almost negatively associated with answer accuracy, thereby serving as a simple yet effective signal for uncertainty quantification. DTC supports both white-box and black-box evaluation using auxiliary models, without explicit training and affecting the generation process. Experiments across multiple model families and six mathematical benchmarks demonstrate improved calibration over probability-based and verbalized baselines. Under white-box evaluation, the count-only estimator achieves an average expected calibration error of 13.0%, compared with 32.7%-42.4% for standard full-sequence confidence methods. In black-box settings, it also improves calibration over the original verbalized scores. For example, mean expected calibration error falls from 32.1%-40.2% to 13.7%-16.3% on DeepSeek-V3.2. These findings provide new insights for improving reasoning uncertainty quantification in large language models. The code is released at https://github.com/szu-tera/DTC.git.

</details>


### [201] [Character Training for Risk-Averse Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38093)
*Arav Dhoot,Punya Syon Pandey,Jamie Johnson,Daniel Tan,Elliott Thornley,David Demitri Africa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究通过角色训练（Character Training）结合模型宪法，成功将绝对风险规避（CARA）偏好植入AI智能体。结果表明，该方法能有效促使未对齐智能体选择更安全策略而非反叛，且具备分布外泛化能力，为缓解AI风险提供了一种可扩展的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防止因资源风险厌恶导致的不对齐AI智能体造成灾难性伤害；旨在验证通过人格特质稳定植入风险偏好的可行性，使智能体倾向于达成交易而非高风险的反叛行为。

Method: 构建描述绝对风险规避（CARA）的模型宪法，并通过基于策略的蒸馏（on-policy distillation）将其注入智能体。采用角色训练方式，不依赖基准测试格式进行直接训练，并调制宪法的不同方面以评估影响因子。

Result: 1. 角色训练的智能体在与基线模型竞争时具有竞争力，且在四种模型中的两种上表现出更好的分布外泛化能力。
2. 令牌预算（token budget）和模型选择是影响角色训练效果、植入风险规避的最关键因素。
3. 验证了人格特质作为植入风险偏好机制的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 角色训练是一种有前景且可扩展的方法，用于植入广泛的性格倾向。利用这种方法可以发挥优势，有效缓解来自不对齐AI智能体的潜在风险。

Abstract: Risk aversion in resources could prevent misaligned AI agents from causing catastrophic harm. Misaligned but risk-averse agents would tend to favor safer strategies like making deals with humans over riskier strategies like rebelling. We train agents to be risk averse through character training, finding that persona traits provide a robust mechanism for instilling risk preferences. To do this, we construct a model constitution describing constant absolute risk aversion (CARA) over an agent's resources and instill it through on-policy distillation. Despite never seeing the benchmark's decision format during training, character-trained models are competitive with baselines trained directly on it, and generalise better than them out of distribution on two of our four models. We also modulate different aspects of the constitution, finding that token budget and model choice are the most influential aspect of character training to instill risk aversion. We conclude from these results that character training is a promising and scalable way to instil broad dispositions, which we can use to our advantage in mitigating risk from misaligned AI agents.

</details>


### [202] [NeuronEye: Query-Guided Visual Concept Activation for Vision-Language Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38098)
*Ruiyu Yan,Bowen Chen,Shaowen Wan,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NeuronEye，一种基于稀疏神经元词汇表的VLM插件框架，通过概念级聚类激活相关视觉证据，在冻结骨干网络单次前向传播中提升特定视觉推理任务精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM将视觉信息编码为密集隐状态，导致物体身份、空间布局和局部属性隐含纠缠，难以根据语言查询隔离和调制特定的视觉证据。

Method: 受生物视觉启发，构建概念级稀疏神经元词汇表；将视觉token分解为由概念聚类组织的超完备稀疏基；利用语言查询激活相关聚类并定位概念表达区域；注入聚焦证据并抑制主导感知方向以保留微弱相关线索；在冻结VLM骨干上执行单次前向传播。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上，CV-Bench整体准确率提升3.1%，距离任务提升9.5%，BLINK多视图提升8.3%；LLaVA-1.6-7B呈现相似趋势。

Conclusion: 稀疏神经元词汇表不仅是事后解释工具，更是实现概念级视觉推理的主动接口，证明了显式解耦视觉信息对提升VLM性能的重要性。

Abstract: Current vision-language models (VLMs) encode visual information in dense hidden states where object identity, spatial layout, and local attributes are implicitly entangled rather than explicitly disentangled, limiting their ability to isolate and modulate the specific visual evidence required by a given language query. Inspired by sparse population coding and top-down modulation in biological vision, we introduce NeuronEye, a plug-in framework that constructs a sparse, concept-level neuron vocabulary from intermediate VLM representations and selectively activates query-relevant visual concepts during inference. NeuronEye decomposes vision-token states into an overcomplete sparse basis organized by concept-level clusters, uses the language query to activate relevant clusters and localize the patches where selected concepts are expressed, and injects the focused evidence back into vision tokens. A complementary suppression mechanism attenuates dominant perceptual directions to preserve weaker but relevant cues. All operations run in a single forward pass over a frozen VLM backbone. On Qwen2.5-VL-7B, NeuronEye raises CV-Bench overall accuracy by +3.1 with gains of +9.5 on Distance, and improves BLINK Multi-view by +8.3, with similar trends on LLaVA-1.6-7B. These results suggest that sparse neuron vocabularies can serve not only as post-hoc interpretability tools but also as active interfaces for concept-level visual reasoning.

</details>


### [203] [Do LLM Agents Execute the Plans They Declare? From Planning-Mode Declaration to Pattern-Specific Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38108)
*Subba Reddy Oota,Francisco Herrera,Jordi Cabot Sagrera,Marcos López de Prado,Shadab Khan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出“规划声明-执行差距”概念，引入Planning-as-Routing框架，通过确定性路由器将任务分派给特定模式的执行器，显著提升了LLM智能体在复杂任务中的执行忠实度与成功率，但模式选择仍需改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有规划器-执行器系统常在计划选择或忠实执行任一阶段失败，且仅凭最终任务成功率无法区分是选择错误还是执行偏差。旨在解决大语言模型在执行长程任务时，计划结构与实际操作脱节的问题。

Method: 提出Planning-as-Routing框架：LLM声明四种规划模式之一（预定义、顺序、层次、搜索），由确定性路由器将任务分发至对应的专用执行器。在四个基准测试和三个LLM上进行验证，对比通用Plan+ReAct与模式特异性执行器的表现。

Result: 1. 通用Plan+ReAct难以维持声明的计划结构（仅22%-45%轨迹保持结构），而模式特异性执行器能强制保持预期结构；2. 不同环境与模型中最优规划模式不同（如ALFWorld中Search最佳，SWE-bench中Hierarchical最佳）；3. 执行优化带来显著收益（ALFWorld成功率从0.48提升至0.92，SWE-bench Verified从0.36提升至0.44）。

Conclusion: 可靠的智能体规划需兼顾有效的模式选择与忠实的执行。Routing方法大幅缩小了执行差距，但当前LLM仍无法可靠地为每个任务自动选择最强规划模式，尽管少量示例（few-shot examples）能在部分场景中改善选择性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) enable agents to solve long-horizon tasks by generating a plan and then executing it in an environment. However, successful planning requires two distinct capabilities: selecting an appropriate plan for the task and executing it faithfully. Existing planner--executor systems can fail at either stage, while final task success alone cannot distinguish selection from execution failures. We therefore study the Plan Declaration--Execution Gap and introduce Planning-as-Routing, where an LLM declares one of four planning modes: Predefined, Sequential, Hierarchical, or Search, and a deterministic router dispatches the task to the corresponding pattern-specific executor. Across four benchmarks and three LLMs, we find three consistent patterns. First, generic Plan+ReAct often fails to preserve declared planning structure, especially for longer plans: across three benchmarks, only (22)--(45%) of trajectories preserve it, whereas pattern-specific executors enforce the intended structure. Second, planning-mode effectiveness varies across environments and models: Search performs best on ALFWorld, Hierarchical on SWE-bench, and the strongest pattern can vary across models within the same benchmark. Third, the largest gains come from execution: pattern-specific executors improve task success from (0.48) to (0.92) on ALFWorld and from (0.36) to (0.44) on SWE-bench Verified over Plan+ReAct. Current LLMs, however, do not reliably select the strongest mode for each task, although few-shot examples improve selection in some benchmark--model combinations. Overall, reliable agent planning requires both effective mode selection and faithful execution: routing substantially closes the execution gap, while task-specific mode selection remains open.

</details>


### [204] [Stochastic World Models for Verifying Vision-Based Neural Feedback Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38120)
*I. Samuel Akinwande,Mykel J. Kochenderfer,Clark Barrett*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用基于物理基础的随机世界模型替代GAN作为感知代理，并结合混合验证流程（ falsification, adaptive refinement, symbolic and backward analyses），在紧急制动基准测试中显著提高了状态空间解析率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于生成对抗网络（GANs）的感知代理存在体积大、复杂场景复现能力差以及难以验证的问题，而视觉神经反馈系统的验证需要一个既能捕捉传感器变化又便于闭环分析的可处理模型。

Method: 训练具有物理基础潜变量的世界模型，其构建基于标准验证器可约束的操作；开发一种结合反例发现（falsification）、自适应细化、符号分析和后向分析的混合验证程序。

Result: 世界模型以高达130倍更少的参数量实现了比GAN代理更忠实地再现未持有帧；在紧急制动基准测试中，该方法解析了全部状态空间的82%（解决了之前SOTA验证器未解决的38部分），并在RGB版本基准中首次报告了解析超过80%状态空间的结果。

Conclusion: 随机世界模型作为感知代理，结合特定的混合验证过程，能够提供更准确且可验证的闭环控制系统模型，有效克服了GAN代理在规模和可验证性上的局限。

Abstract: Verifying a vision-based neural feedback system requires a model of the observations its controller acts upon. Such a model must capture the variation the sensor produces, while remaining tractable for closed-loop analysis. Generative adversarial networks (GANs) have served as perception surrogates, but they are large, reproduce complex scenes poorly, and are hard to verify. We explore stochastic world models as a richer class of perception surrogates. We train a world model with physically grounded latents, built from operations that standard verifiers bound. It reproduces held-out frames more faithfully than GAN surrogates with up to 130 times as many parameters. To verify these surrogates, we develop a procedure that combines falsification, adaptive refinement, symbolic, and backward analyses. On an emergency braking benchmark with a GAN surrogate, our procedure resolves the entire state space, 38% of which the state-of-the-art verifier left unresolved. On the RGB version of the benchmark, where no verification results have previously been reported, our procedure resolves over 80% of the state space with a world model surrogate.

</details>


### [205] [AdviSD: Learning to Advise Frontier LLMs via Targeted Multi-Turn Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38142)
*Rishabh Agrawal,Hejie Cui,Shasha Li,Shanchan Wu,Sercan Ö. Arık*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AdviSD方法，通过选择性自我蒸馏优化小型可训练顾问模型，避免无效反馈干扰，提升对冻结语言模型执行器的引导效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在共享参数模型中，证明某些“看似合理但实际不改变执行结果”的修正反馈若用于学习，会限制顾问模型的进步；若保留较少此类反馈，可提升最终性能。

Method: Advisor Self-Distillation (AdviSD)：结合基于结果的强化学习与自蒸馏。顾问反思并提出修正，评估带/不带建议时执行器响应的差异幅度，据此有选择地选取决策进行监督学习。无需执行器似然值或额外rollouts。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B作为顾问指导Gemini和Claude的实验显示：AdviSD在BFCL-v3上较advisor-GRPO提升4.2-6.4个百分点，在EnvScaler上提升3.9-5.1分。具备领域外泛化能力及跨模型版本/族迁移能力，优于随机选择策略。

Conclusion: AdviSD通过选择性利用反馈信息有效避免了低质量修正对学习过程的负面影响，显著提升了顾问模型的指导性能与泛化能力，验证了所提选择规则的优越性。

Abstract: A small trainable advisor can steer a frozen language-model executor using natural-language advice. In addition to learning from task rewards, the advisor can use feedback from completed interactions to improve its advice.
